+type EffectivePlayerView = Readonly<{
+  albumSlug: string;
+  album: AlbumPlayerBundle["album"];
+  tracks: PlayerTrack[];
+}>;
+
+function resolveAccessIdentityKey(
+  params: Readonly<{
+    clerkAuthLoaded: boolean;
+    isSignedIn: boolean | undefined;
+    userId: string | null | undefined;
+    sessionId: string | null | undefined;
+  }>,
+): string {
+  if (!params.clerkAuthLoaded) return "clerk:loading";
+  if (!params.isSignedIn) return "clerk:anonymous";
+
+  return `clerk:user:${params.userId ?? ""}:session:${params.sessionId ?? ""}`;
+}
+
+function resolveEffectivePlayerView(
+  params: Readonly<{
+    bundle: AlbumPlayerBundle;
+    stableView: StableView | null;
+    isBrowsingAlbum: boolean;
+  }>,
+): EffectivePlayerView {
+  const { bundle, stableView, isBrowsingAlbum } = params;
+  const shouldUseStableView = Boolean(
+    isBrowsingAlbum &&
+    stableView?.album &&
+    (!bundle.album || bundle.tracks.length === 0),
+  );
+
+  if (shouldUseStableView && stableView) {
+    return {
+      albumSlug: stableView.albumSlug,
+      album: stableView.album,
+      tracks: stableView.tracks,
+    };
+  }
+
+  return {
+    albumSlug: bundle.albumSlug,
+    album: bundle.album,
+    tracks: bundle.tracks,
+  };
+}
+
+function accessStatesEqual(
+  left: AccessState | null,
+  right: AccessState | null,
+): boolean {
+  if (!left || !right) return left === right;
+  return JSON.stringify(left) === JSON.stringify(right);
+}
+
+function mergeAccessState(
+  previous: AccessState | null,
+  next: AccessState | null,
+): AccessState | null {
+  return accessStatesEqual(previous, next) ? previous : next;
+}
+
+function isAuthOrLegacyCapCode(code: string): boolean {
+  return (
+    code === "AUTH_REQUIRED" ||
+    code === "CAP_REACHED" ||
+    code === "ANON_CAP_REACHED"
+  );
+}
+
+function hasPlaybackIntentForAlbum(
+  player: PlayerApi,
+  pendingInAlbum: boolean,
+): boolean {
+  return (
+    player.playRequestPending ||
+    player.intent === "play" ||
+    player.status === "loading" ||
+    player.status === "playing" ||
+    player.status === "paused" ||
+    pendingInAlbum
+  );
+}
+
+function reconcilePlayerWithAccessDecision(
+  params: Readonly<{
+    player: PlayerApi;
+    decision: AccessState;
+    recordingIds: ReadonlySet<string>;
+    albumKey: string | null;
+  }>,
+): void {
+  const { player, decision, recordingIds, albumKey } = params;
+
+  if (decision.allowed) {
+    if (player.lastError) player.clearError();
+    return;
+  }
+
+  const currentInAlbum = Boolean(
+    player.current?.recordingId && recordingIds.has(player.current.recordingId),
+  );
+  const pendingInAlbum = Boolean(
+    player.pendingRecordingId && recordingIds.has(player.pendingRecordingId),
+  );
+  const queueIsAlbum = Boolean(albumKey && player.queueContextId === albumKey);
+  const hasIntent = hasPlaybackIntentForAlbum(player, pendingInAlbum);
+  const shouldBlockAlbum =
+    currentInAlbum || pendingInAlbum || (queueIsAlbum && hasIntent);
+
+  const code = typeof decision.code === "string" ? decision.code.trim() : "";
+  if (!shouldBlockAlbum || (isAuthOrLegacyCapCode(code) && !hasIntent)) return;
+
+  try {
+    player.pause();
+  } catch {
+    // ignore
+  }
+
+  if (player.status === "loading") {
+    player.setStatusExternal("idle");
+  }
+}
+
+function accessFailureFallback(catalogueId: string): AccessState {
+  return {
+    forCatalogueId: catalogueId,
+    allowed: true,
+    embargoed: false,
+    releaseAt: null,
+    code: "ACCESS_CHECK_ERROR",
+    reason: "Access check failed (client).",
+    corr: null,
+    shareTokenAccess: null,
+    sharePlaybackContext: null,
+    sharePlaybackScopeId: null,
+  };
+}
+
+function resolveAccessForAlbum(
+  catalogueId: string | null,
+  access: AccessState | null,
+): AccessState | null {
+  if (!catalogueId) return null;
+  if (access?.forCatalogueId !== catalogueId) return null;
+  return access;
+}
+
+function isPlayerPlayingish(player: PlayerApi): boolean {
+  return (
+    player.status === "playing" ||
+    player.status === "loading" ||
+    player.playRequestPending
+  );
+}
+
+type PlaybackAccessUiState = Readonly<{
+  accessKnown: boolean;
+  isPlaybackGate: boolean;
+  canPlay: boolean;
+  canAttemptPlay: boolean;
+}>;
+
+function derivePlaybackAccessUiState(
+  tracks: readonly PlayerTrack[],
+  accessForAlbum: AccessState | null,
+): PlaybackAccessUiState {
+  const accessKnown = accessForAlbum !== null;
+  const hasPlayableTracks = tracks.length > 0;
+  const isPlaybackGate = Boolean(
+    hasPlayableTracks &&
+    accessKnown &&
+    accessForAlbum?.allowed === false &&
+    isPlaybackGateCode(accessForAlbum.code),
+  );
+  const canPlay = Boolean(
+    hasPlayableTracks && accessKnown && accessForAlbum?.allowed !== false,
+  );
+
+  return {
+    accessKnown,
+    isPlaybackGate,
+    canPlay,
+    canAttemptPlay: canPlay || isPlaybackGate,
+  };
+}
+
+type EmbargoUiState = Readonly<{
+  releaseAtMs: number;
+  showEmbargo: boolean;
+  showShareTokenEmbargoAccess: boolean;
+}>;
+
+function deriveEmbargoUiState(
+  params: Readonly<{
+    embargo: NonNullable<AlbumPlayerBundle["album"]>["embargo"];
+    accessForAlbum: AccessState | null;
+    shareToken: string | null;
+  }>,
+): EmbargoUiState {
+  const releaseAtMs = params.embargo?.releaseAt
+    ? Date.parse(params.embargo.releaseAt)
+    : Number.NaN;
+  const accessResolved = params.accessForAlbum !== null;
+  const accessAllowsEmbargoBypass = params.accessForAlbum?.allowed === true;
+  const showEmbargo = Boolean(
+    params.embargo?.embargoed &&
+    Number.isFinite(releaseAtMs) &&
+    !accessAllowsEmbargoBypass &&
+    (!params.shareToken || accessResolved),
+  );
+  const showShareTokenEmbargoAccess = Boolean(
+    params.embargo?.embargoed &&
+    params.accessForAlbum?.allowed === true &&
+    params.accessForAlbum.shareTokenAccess,
+  );
+
+  return { releaseAtMs, showEmbargo, showShareTokenEmbargoAccess };
+}

+function isAlbumPlaybackActive(
+  albumKey: string | null,
+  queueContextId: string | undefined,
+): boolean {
+  return Boolean(albumKey && queueContextId === albumKey);
+}
+
+type AlbumTransportState = Readonly<{
+  albumIdx: number;
+  prevDisabled: boolean;
+  nextDisabled: boolean;
+}>;
+
+function deriveAlbumTransportState(
+  params: Readonly<{
+    currentRecordingId: string;
+    tracks: readonly PlayerTrack[];
+    transportLock: boolean;
+  }>,
+): AlbumTransportState {
+  const albumIdx = params.currentRecordingId
+    ? params.tracks.findIndex(
+        (track) => track.recordingId === params.currentRecordingId,
+      )
+    : -1;
+
+  if (albumIdx < 0) {
+    return { albumIdx, prevDisabled: true, nextDisabled: true };
+  }
+
+  return {
+    albumIdx,
+    prevDisabled: params.transportLock || albumIdx === 0,
+    nextDisabled: params.transportLock || albumIdx === params.tracks.length - 1,
+  };
+}
+
+function isCoarsePointerDevice(): boolean {
+  if (typeof window === "undefined") return false;
+
+  try {
+    return window.matchMedia?.("(pointer: coarse)").matches ?? false;
+  } catch {
+    return "ontouchstart" in window;
+  }
+}
+
+function trackRowColors(
+  canPlay: boolean,
+  isCurrent: boolean,
+): Readonly<{ titleColor: string; subColor: string }> {
+  if (!canPlay) {
+    return {
+      titleColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.38)",
+      subColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.32)",
+    };
+  }
+
+  if (isCurrent) {
+    return {
+      titleColor: "var(--accent)",
+      subColor: "color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 70%, rgba(255,255,255,0.70))",
+    };
+  }
+
+  return {
+    titleColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.92)",
+    subColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.70)",
+  };
+}
+
+function trackRowRadius(index: number, trackCount: number): string {
+  if (index === 0) return "14px 14px 0 0";
+  if (index === trackCount - 1) return "0 0 14px 14px";
+  return "0";
+}
+
+type TrackRowView = Readonly<{
+  isCurrent: boolean;
+  shimmerTitle: boolean;
+  isNowPlaying: boolean;
+  titleColor: string;
+  subColor: string;
+  restingBackground: string;
+  rowRadius: string;
+  dataReason: string;
+  cursor: "pointer" | "default";
+  opacity: number;
+}>;
+
+function deriveTrackRowView(
+  params: Readonly<{
+    track: PlayerTrack;
+    index: number;
+    trackCount: number;
+    currentRecordingId: string | undefined;
+    pendingRecordingId: string | undefined;
+    playerStatus: PlayerApi["status"];
+    playerIntent: PlayerApi["intent"];
+    loadingReason: PlayerApi["loadingReason"];
+    canPlay: boolean;
+    canAttemptPlay: boolean;
+    isSelected: boolean;
+  }>,
+): TrackRowView {
+  const isCurrent = params.currentRecordingId === params.track.recordingId;
+  const isPending = params.pendingRecordingId === params.track.recordingId;
+  const shimmerTitle =
+    params.playerStatus === "loading" && isPending && !isCurrent;
+  const isNowPlaying = Boolean(
+    isCurrent &&
+    (params.playerStatus === "playing" ||
+      params.playerStatus === "loading" ||
+      params.playerIntent === "play"),
+  );
+  const { titleColor, subColor } = trackRowColors(params.canPlay, isCurrent);
+  const baseBackground = params.isSelected
+    ? "rgba(255,255,255,0.14)"
+    : "transparent";
+  const restingBackground =
+    isCurrent && !params.isSelected ? "transparent" : baseBackground;
+  const dataReason =
+    isCurrent && params.playerStatus === "loading"
+      ? (params.loadingReason ?? "")
+      : "";
+
+  return {
+    isCurrent,
+    shimmerTitle,
+    isNowPlaying,
+    titleColor,
+    subColor,
+    restingBackground,
+    rowRadius: trackRowRadius(params.index, params.trackCount),
+    dataReason,
+    cursor: params.canAttemptPlay ? "pointer" : "default",
+    opacity: params.canPlay ? 1 : 0.75,
+  };
+}
+
+function trackPlayLabel(track: PlayerTrack, index: number): string {
+  const title = track.title?.trim();
+  const subject = title || `track ${index + 1}`;
+  return `Play ${subject}`;
+}

+function trackRowActionLabel(

+}

+function EmbargoNotices(

+    showEmbargo: boolean;

+    embargoNote: string | null | undefined;

+function TransportControls(
+  props: Readonly<{
+    prevDisabled: boolean;
+    nextDisabled: boolean;
+    canAttemptPlay: boolean;
+    canPlay: boolean;
+    playLock: boolean;
+    playingThisAlbum: boolean;
+    onDownload: () => void;
+    onPrevious: () => void;
+    onTogglePlay: () => void;
+    onNext: () => void;
+    onShare: () => void;
+  }>,
+) {
+  const playLabel = props.playingThisAlbum ? "Pause" : "Play";
+  const playClick =
+    props.canAttemptPlay && !props.playLock ? props.onTogglePlay : undefined;
+  const playCursor = props.canAttemptPlay ? "pointer" : "default";
+  const playOpacity = props.canAttemptPlay ? 1 : 0.55;
+  const playShadow = props.playingThisAlbum
+    ? "0 18px 50px rgba(0,0,0,0.35)"
+    : "0 18px 50px rgba(0,0,0,0.30)";
+
+  return (
+    <div className="afTransportRow">
+      <div className="afTransportBadgeSlot" aria-hidden="true" />
+
+      <div className="afTransportControls">
+        <IconCircleBtn label="Download" onClick={props.onDownload}>
+          <DownloadIcon />
+        </IconCircleBtn>
+
+        <IconCircleBtn
+          label="Previous"
+          disabled={props.prevDisabled}
+          onClick={props.onPrevious}
+        >
+          <PrevIcon />
+        </IconCircleBtn>
+
+        <div style={{ position: "relative", width: 64, height: 64 }}>
+          <button
+            type="button"
+            onClick={playClick}
+            disabled={!props.canAttemptPlay || props.playLock}
+            aria-label={playLabel}
+            title={playLabel}
+            style={{
+              width: 64,
+              height: 64,
+              borderRadius: 999,
+              border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.12)",
+              background: "rgba(245,245,245,0.95)",
+              color: "rgba(0,0,0,0.92)",
+              display: "grid",
+              placeItems: "center",
+              cursor: playCursor,
+              opacity: playOpacity,
+              boxShadow: playShadow,
+              transform: "translateZ(0)",
+              position: "relative",
+              zIndex: 2,
+            }}
+          >
+            <PlayPauseBig playing={props.playingThisAlbum} />
+          </button>
+
+          {props.canPlay ? (
+            <div
+              style={{
+                position: "absolute",
+                inset: -5,
+                zIndex: 1,
+                pointerEvents: "none",
+                overflow: "visible",
+                isolation: "isolate",
+                transform: "translateZ(0)",
+                display: "grid",
+                placeItems: "center",
+              }}
+            >
+              <PatternRingGlow
+                size={64}
+                ringPx={1}
+                glowPx={2}
+                blurPx={4}
+                opacity={0.45}
+                seed={913}
+              />
+            </div>
+          ) : null}
+        </div>
+
+        <IconCircleBtn
+          label="Next"
+          disabled={props.nextDisabled}
+          onClick={props.onNext}
+        >
+          <NextIcon />
+        </IconCircleBtn>
+
+        <IconCircleBtn label="Share" onClick={props.onShare}>
+          <ShareIcon />
+        </IconCircleBtn>
+      </div>
+
+      <div aria-hidden="true" />
+    </div>
+  );
+}
+
+function TrackIndexCell(
+  props: Readonly<{
+    index: number;
+    track: PlayerTrack;
+    isNowPlaying: boolean;
+    canAttemptPlay: boolean;
+    isCurrent: boolean;
+    subColor: string;
+    onInlinePlay: (track: PlayerTrack) => void;
+  }>,
+) {
+  let content: React.ReactNode = (
+    <span className="afTrackIndexNumber">{props.index + 1}</span>
+  );
+
+  if (props.isNowPlaying) {
+    content = <NowPlayingPip />;
+  } else if (props.canAttemptPlay && !props.isCurrent) {
+    content = (
+      <span className="afTrackIndexSwap">
+        <span className="afTrackIndexNumber">{props.index + 1}</span>
+        <button
+          type="button"
+          className="afTrackInlinePlay"
+          aria-label={trackPlayLabel(props.track, props.index)}
+          title="Play"
+          onClick={(event) => {
+            event.preventDefault();
+            event.stopPropagation();
+            props.onInlinePlay(props.track);
+          }}
+        >
+          <TrackInlinePlayIcon />
+        </button>
+      </span>
+    );
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <div
+      style={{
+        fontSize: 12,
+        opacity: 0.9,
+        display: "flex",
+        alignItems: "center",
+        gap: 6,
+        color: props.subColor,
+        paddingLeft: 12,
+        justifyContent: "flex-start",
+        position: "relative",
+        zIndex: 1,
+        pointerEvents: "none",
+      }}
+    >
+      {content}
+    </div>
+  );
+}

    1 | // web/app/(site)/PlayerHost.tsx
    2 | "use client";
    3 | 
    4 | import React from "react";
    5 | import { PlayerStateProvider } from "@/app/home/player/PlayerState";
    6 | import AudioEngine from "@/app/home/player/AudioEngine";
    7 | import TrackTitleSync from "@/app/home/player/TrackTitleSync";
    8 | import StageInlineHost from "@/app/home/player/StageInlineHost";
    9 | 
   10 | export default function PlayerHost({
   11 |   children,
   12 | }: {
   13 |   children: React.ReactNode;
   14 | }) {
   15 |   React.useEffect(() => {
   16 |     if (
   17 |       typeof window === "undefined" ||
   18 |       process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_NAV_DEBUG !== "1"
   19 |     ) {
   20 |       return;
   21 |     }
   22 | 
   23 |     const tag = "[NAV]";
   24 |     const origPush = window.history.pushState.bind(window.history);
   25 |     const origReplace = window.history.replaceState.bind(window.history);
   26 | 
   27 |     const log = (
   28 |       kind: "pushState" | "replaceState",
   29 |       url?: string | URL | null,
   30 |     ) => {
   31 |       try {
   32 |         const href = String(url ?? "");
   33 |         const from = window.location.pathname;
   34 | 
   35 |         const toPath = href.startsWith("http")
   36 |           ? new URL(href).pathname
   37 |           : (href.split("?")[0] || "").trim();
   38 | 
   39 |         if (
   40 |           (from === "/portal" && toPath === "/journal") ||
   41 |           href.includes("/journal") ||
   42 |           href.includes("/portal") ||
   43 |           // legacy (avoid breaking old in-flight links/history)
   44 |           href.includes("/posts") ||
   45 |           href.includes("/extras")
   46 |         ) {
   47 |           console.log(tag, kind, { from, href }, new Error().stack);
   48 |         }
   49 |       } catch {
   50 |         // ignore
   51 |       }
   52 |     };
   53 | 
   54 |     window.history.pushState = function (
   55 |       data: unknown,
   56 |       unused: string,
   57 |       url?: string | URL | null,
   58 |     ): void {
   59 |       log("pushState", url);
   60 |       origPush(data, unused, url);
   61 |     };
   62 | 
   63 |     window.history.replaceState = function (
   64 |       data: unknown,
   65 |       unused: string,
   66 |       url?: string | URL | null,
   67 |     ): void {
   68 |       log("replaceState", url);
   69 |       origReplace(data, unused, url);
   70 |     };
   71 | 
   72 |     const onPop = () => {
   73 |       console.log(tag, "popstate", { href: window.location.href });
   74 |     };
   75 |     window.addEventListener("popstate", onPop);
   76 | 
   77 |     return () => {
   78 |       window.history.pushState = origPush;
   79 |       window.history.replaceState = origReplace;
   80 |       window.removeEventListener("popstate", onPop);
   81 |     };
   82 |   }, []);
   83 | 
   84 |   return (
   85 |     <PlayerStateProvider>
   86 |       <AudioEngine />
   87 |       <StageInlineHost />
   88 |       <TrackTitleSync fallbackLeaf="God Defend" mode="track" />
   89 |       {children}
   90 |     </PlayerStateProvider>
   91 |   );
   92 | }

===== COMPLETE FILE: app/home/MiniPlayerHost.tsx =====
    1 | "use client";
    2 | 
    3 | import React from "react";
    4 | import { usePlayer } from "@/app/home/player/PlayerState";
    5 | import MiniPlayer from "./player/MiniPlayer";
    6 | 
    7 | export default function MiniPlayerHost(props: { onExpand: () => void }) {
    8 |   const { onExpand } = props;
    9 |   const p = usePlayer();
   10 | 
   11 |   const intent = p.intent;
   12 |   const status = p.status;
   13 |   const playRequestPending = p.playRequestPending;
   14 |   const current = p.current;
   15 |   const queueLen = p.queue.length;
   16 | 
   17 |   const [miniActive, setMiniActive] = React.useState(() => {
   18 |     if (typeof window === "undefined") return false;
   19 |     return window.sessionStorage.getItem("af:miniActive") === "1";
   20 |   });
   21 | 
   22 |   React.useEffect(() => {
   23 |     const shouldActivate =
   24 |       playRequestPending ||
   25 |       intent === "play" ||
   26 |       status === "playing" ||
   27 |       status === "paused" ||
   28 |       Boolean(current) ||
   29 |       queueLen > 0;
   30 | 
   31 |     if (!miniActive && shouldActivate) {
   32 |       setMiniActive(true);
   33 |       try {
   34 |         window.sessionStorage.setItem("af:miniActive", "1");
   35 |       } catch {}
   36 |     }
   37 |   }, [miniActive, intent, status, playRequestPending, current, queueLen]);
   38 | 
   39 |   if (!miniActive) return null;
   40 | 
   41 |   return (
   42 |     <MiniPlayer
   43 |       onExpand={onExpand}
   44 |       artworkUrl={p.queueContextArtworkUrl ?? null}
   45 |     />
   46 |   );
   47 | }

   90 | type HlsJsConstructor = typeof import("hls.js").default;

   92 | let hlsConstructorCache: HlsJsConstructor | null = null;
   93 | let hlsConstructorPromise: Promise<HlsJsConstructor> | null = null;
   94 | 
   95 | function loadHlsConstructor(): Promise<HlsJsConstructor> {
   96 |   if (hlsConstructorCache) {
   97 |     return Promise.resolve(hlsConstructorCache);
   98 |   }
   99 | 
  100 |   if (!hlsConstructorPromise) {
  101 |     hlsConstructorPromise = import("hls.js")
  102 |       .then((module) => {
  103 |         hlsConstructorCache = module.default;
  104 |         return module.default;
  105 |       })
  106 |       .catch((error: unknown) => {
  107 |         hlsConstructorPromise = null;
  108 |         throw error;
  109 |       });
  110 |   }
  111 | 
  112 |   return hlsConstructorPromise;
  113 | }

  140 | function shouldUseNativeHls(a: HTMLMediaElement): boolean {
  141 |   if (typeof navigator === "undefined") return false;
  142 | 
  143 |   const ua = navigator.userAgent;
  144 |   const isAndroid = /Android/i.test(ua);
  145 |   const isChrome = /Chrome|CriOS|Chromium/i.test(ua);
  146 |   const isSafari = /Safari/i.test(ua) && !isChrome;
  147 | 
  148 |   if (isAndroid) return false;
  149 | 
  150 |   return isSafari && a.canPlayType("application/vnd.apple.mpegurl") !== "";
  151 | }

  153 | const STATIC_M4A_STANDBY_MIN_BUFFER_AHEAD_SEC = 20;

  157 | // decks. Set this to 0 to retain strictly native-ended handoff behaviour.

  161 | function isAppleMobileWebKit(): boolean {
  162 |   if (typeof navigator === "undefined") return false;
  163 | 
  164 |   const ua = navigator.userAgent;
  165 |   const isAppleHandheld = /iPad|iPhone|iPod/i.test(ua);
  166 |   const isTouchCapableIpadDesktopUa =
  167 |     navigator.platform === "MacIntel" && navigator.maxTouchPoints > 1;
  168 | 
  169 |   return (
  170 |     (isAppleHandheld || isTouchCapableIpadDesktopUa) && /AppleWebKit/i.test(ua)
  171 |   );
  172 | }

  174 | function shouldUseStaticM4a(): boolean {
  175 |   return isAppleMobileWebKit();
  176 | }
  177 | 
  178 | function bufferedAheadSeconds(media: HTMLMediaElement): number {
  179 |   try {
  180 |     const currentTime = Math.max(0, media.currentTime);
  181 | 
  182 |     for (let index = 0; index < media.buffered.length; index += 1) {
  183 |       const start = media.buffered.start(index);
  184 |       const end = media.buffered.end(index);
  185 | 
  186 |       if (currentTime >= start && currentTime <= end) {
  187 |         return Math.max(0, end - currentTime);
  188 |       }
  189 |     }
  190 | 
  191 |     if (media.buffered.length > 0) {
  192 |       return Math.max(
  193 |         0,
  194 |         media.buffered.end(media.buffered.length - 1) - currentTime,
  195 |       );
  196 |     }
  197 |   } catch {}
  198 | 
  199 |   return 0;
  200 | }
  201 | 
  202 | function readFiniteMediaDurationMs(media: HTMLMediaElement): number {
  203 |   const durationSec = media.duration;
  204 | 
  205 |   return Number.isFinite(durationSec) && durationSec > 0
  206 |     ? Math.round(durationSec * 1000)
  207 |     : 0;
  208 | }

  242 | function getMediaSessionArtwork(
  243 |   rawArtworkUrl: string | null | undefined,
  244 | ): MediaSessionArtwork[] {
  245 |   const artworkUrl = (rawArtworkUrl ?? "").trim();
  246 | 
  247 |   if (!artworkUrl) return [];
  248 | 
  249 |   let sourceUrl: URL;
  250 | 
  251 |   try {
  252 |     sourceUrl = new URL(artworkUrl);
  253 |   } catch {
  254 |     return [];
  255 |   }
  256 | 
  257 |   // The album artwork currently comes from Sanity's CDN. Supplying several
  258 |   // correctly described JPEG variants gives iOS a clean, native-friendly
  259 |   // choice for Now Playing and the lock screen.
  260 |   if (sourceUrl.hostname !== "cdn.sanity.io") {
  261 |     return [{ src: sourceUrl.toString() }];
  262 |   }
  263 | 
  264 |   const sizes = [96, 128, 192, 256, 384, 512, 1024];
  265 | 
  266 |   return sizes.map((size) => {
  267 |     const url = new URL(sourceUrl.toString());
  268 | 
  269 |     url.searchParams.set("w", String(size));
  270 |     url.searchParams.set("h", String(size));
  271 |     url.searchParams.set("fit", "crop");
  272 |     url.searchParams.set("crop", "center");
  273 |     url.searchParams.set("fm", "jpg");
  274 |     url.searchParams.set("q", "85");
  275 |     url.searchParams.set("cs", "srgb");
  276 | 
  277 |     return {
  278 |       src: url.toString(),
  279 |       sizes: `${size}x${size}`,
  280 |       type: "image/jpeg",
  281 |     };
  282 |   });
  283 | }

  293 | function shouldSendAudioDebugEvent(event: string): boolean {

  314 | const AUDIO_DEBUG_STORAGE_KEY = "af:audio-debug:v1";
  315 | const AUDIO_DEBUG_STORAGE_LIMIT = 240;
  316 | 
  317 | const audioDebugMemoryBuffer: AudioDebugEvent[] = [];
  318 | let audioDebugFlushTimer: number | null = null;
  319 | let audioDebugFlushInFlight = false;
  320 | 
  321 | function isStoredAudioDebugEvent(value: unknown): value is AudioDebugEvent {
  322 |   if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return false;
  323 | 
  324 |   const candidate = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  325 | 
  326 |   return (
  327 |     typeof candidate.t === "number" &&
  328 |     Number.isFinite(candidate.t) &&
  329 |     typeof candidate.event === "string"
  330 |   );
  331 | }
  332 | 
  333 | function readPersistedAudioDebugEvents(): AudioDebugEvent[] | null {
  334 |   if (typeof window === "undefined") return null;
  335 | 
  336 |   try {
  337 |     const raw = window.sessionStorage.getItem(AUDIO_DEBUG_STORAGE_KEY);
  338 | 
  339 |     if (!raw?.trim()) return [];
  340 | 
  341 |     const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(raw);
  342 | 
  343 |     if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) return [];
  344 | 
  345 |     return parsed
  346 |       .filter(isStoredAudioDebugEvent)
  347 |       .slice(-AUDIO_DEBUG_STORAGE_LIMIT);
  348 |   } catch {
  349 |     return null;
  350 |   }
  351 | }
  352 | 
  353 | function writePersistedAudioDebugEvents(events: AudioDebugEvent[]): boolean {
  354 |   if (typeof window === "undefined") return false;
  355 | 
  356 |   try {
  357 |     window.sessionStorage.setItem(
  358 |       AUDIO_DEBUG_STORAGE_KEY,
  359 |       JSON.stringify(events.slice(-AUDIO_DEBUG_STORAGE_LIMIT)),
  360 |     );
  361 |     return true;
  362 |   } catch {
  363 |     return false;
  364 |   }
  365 | }
  366 | 
  367 | function appendAudioDebugEvent(event: AudioDebugEvent): void {
  368 |   const persisted = readPersistedAudioDebugEvents();
  369 | 
  370 |   if (
  371 |     persisted !== null &&
  372 |     writePersistedAudioDebugEvents([...persisted, event])
  373 |   ) {
  374 |     return;
  375 |   }
  376 | 
  377 |   audioDebugMemoryBuffer.push(event);
  378 | 
  379 |   if (audioDebugMemoryBuffer.length > AUDIO_DEBUG_STORAGE_LIMIT) {
  380 |     audioDebugMemoryBuffer.splice(
  381 |       0,
  382 |       audioDebugMemoryBuffer.length - AUDIO_DEBUG_STORAGE_LIMIT,
  383 |     );
  384 |   }
  385 | }
  386 | 
  387 | function takeAudioDebugEvents(): AudioDebugEvent[] {
  388 |   const persisted = readPersistedAudioDebugEvents();
  389 | 
  390 |   if (persisted !== null) {
  391 |     if (writePersistedAudioDebugEvents([])) {
  392 |       return persisted;
  393 |     }
  394 | 
  395 |     return persisted;
  396 |   }
  397 | 
  398 |   return audioDebugMemoryBuffer.splice(0, audioDebugMemoryBuffer.length);
  399 | }
  400 | 
  401 | function restoreAudioDebugEvents(events: AudioDebugEvent[]): void {
  402 |   if (!events.length) return;
  403 | 
  404 |   const persisted = readPersistedAudioDebugEvents();
  405 | 
  406 |   if (
  407 |     persisted !== null &&
  408 |     writePersistedAudioDebugEvents([...events, ...persisted])
  409 |   ) {
  410 |     return;
  411 |   }
  412 | 
  413 |   audioDebugMemoryBuffer.unshift(...events);
  414 | 
  415 |   if (audioDebugMemoryBuffer.length > AUDIO_DEBUG_STORAGE_LIMIT) {
  416 |     audioDebugMemoryBuffer.splice(
  417 |       AUDIO_DEBUG_STORAGE_LIMIT,
  418 |       audioDebugMemoryBuffer.length - AUDIO_DEBUG_STORAGE_LIMIT,
  419 |     );
  420 |   }
  421 | }
  422 | 
  423 | function flushAudioDebugSoon(force = false): void {
  424 |   if (!audioDebugEnabled()) return;
  425 | 
  426 |   const flush = () => {
  427 |     audioDebugFlushTimer = null;
  428 | 
  429 |     if (audioDebugFlushInFlight) return;
  430 | 
  431 |     const events = takeAudioDebugEvents();
  432 |     if (!events.length) return;
  433 | 
  434 |     audioDebugFlushInFlight = true;
  435 | 
  436 |     const restore = () => {
  437 |       restoreAudioDebugEvents(events);
  438 |     };
  439 | 
  440 |     try {
  441 |       void fetch("/api/playback/debug", {
  442 |         method: "POST",
  443 |         headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  444 |         body: JSON.stringify({
  445 |           sessionId: audioDebugSessionId,
  446 |           href: window.location.href,
  447 |           events,
  448 |         }),
  449 |         keepalive: true,
  450 |       })
  451 |         .then((response) => {
  452 |           if (!response.ok) {
  453 |             throw new Error(`Audio debug request failed (${response.status})`);
  454 |           }
  455 |         })
  456 |         .catch(restore)
  457 |         .finally(() => {
  458 |           audioDebugFlushInFlight = false;
  459 | 
  460 |           if (readPersistedAudioDebugEvents()?.length) {
  461 |             flushAudioDebugSoon();
  462 |           }
  463 |         });
  464 |     } catch {
  465 |       restore();
  466 |       audioDebugFlushInFlight = false;
  467 |     }
  468 |   };
  469 | 
  470 |   if (force) {
  471 |     if (audioDebugFlushTimer != null) {
  472 |       window.clearTimeout(audioDebugFlushTimer);
  473 |       audioDebugFlushTimer = null;
  474 |     }
  475 | 
  476 |     flush();
  477 |     return;
  478 |   }
  479 | 
  480 |   if (audioDebugFlushTimer != null) return;
  481 | 
  482 |   audioDebugFlushTimer = window.setTimeout(flush, 5000);
  483 | }
  484 | 
  485 | function sendAudioDebug(payload: {
  486 |   event: string;
  487 |   albumId?: string | null;
  488 |   recordingId?: string | null;
  489 |   playbackId?: string | null;
  490 |   source?: string | null;
  491 |   detail?: string | null;
  492 | }): void {
  493 |   if (!audioDebugEnabled()) return;
  494 |   if (!shouldSendAudioDebugEvent(payload.event)) return;
  495 | 
  496 |   const event: AudioDebugEvent = {
  497 |     t: Math.floor(performance.now()),
  498 |     ...payload,
  499 |   };
  500 | 
  501 |   appendAudioDebugEvent(event);
  502 | 
  503 |   if (audioDebugVerboseEnabled()) {
  504 |     try {
  505 |       console.info("[audio-debug]", {
  506 |         sessionId: audioDebugSessionId,
  507 |         ...event,
  508 |       });
  509 |     } catch {}
  510 |   }
  511 | 
  512 |   // Batch diagnostics after the playback-critical call stack has completed.
  513 |   // Immediate per-event fetches can perturb timing-sensitive mobile playback.
  514 |   flushAudioDebugSoon();
  515 | }
  516 | 
  517 | function setMediaSessionPositionStateSafe(args: {
  518 |   durationSec: number;
  519 |   positionSec: number;
  520 |   playbackRate?: number;
  521 | }): void {
  522 |   if (!hasMediaSession()) return;
  523 |   if (typeof navigator.mediaSession.setPositionState !== "function") return;
  524 | 
  525 |   const duration = Number.isFinite(args.durationSec)
  526 |     ? Math.max(0, args.durationSec)
  527 |     : 0;
  528 |   const position = Number.isFinite(args.positionSec)
  529 |     ? Math.max(0, Math.min(args.positionSec, duration || args.positionSec))
  530 |     : 0;
  531 |   const playbackRate =
  532 |     typeof args.playbackRate === "number" &&
  533 |     Number.isFinite(args.playbackRate) &&
  534 |     args.playbackRate > 0
  535 |       ? args.playbackRate
  536 |       : 1;
  537 | 
  538 |   try {
  539 |     navigator.mediaSession.setPositionState({
  540 |       duration,
  541 |       position,
  542 |       playbackRate,
  543 |     });
  544 |   } catch {}
  545 | }
  546 | 
  547 | function otherDeck(deckId: DeckId): DeckId {
  548 |   return deckId === "a" ? "b" : "a";
  549 | }
  550 | 
  551 | function normalizeAlbumId(raw: string | null | undefined): string {
  552 |   let s = (raw ?? "").trim();
  553 |   while (s.startsWith("alb:")) s = s.slice(4);
  554 |   return s.trim();
  555 | }
  556 | 
  557 | type SharePlaybackAttribution = {
  558 |   context: string;
  559 |   scopeId: string;
  560 | };
  561 | 
  562 | function readQueueSharePlaybackAttribution(queue: {
  563 |   queueSharePlaybackContext?: string | null;
  564 |   queueSharePlaybackScopeId?: string | null;
  565 | }): SharePlaybackAttribution | null {
  566 |   const context = queue.queueSharePlaybackContext?.trim() ?? "";
  567 |   const scopeId = queue.queueSharePlaybackScopeId?.trim() ?? "";
  568 | 
  569 |   if (!context.startsWith("stpc1.") || context.length > 900) {
  570 |     return null;
  571 |   }
  572 | 
  573 |   if (!/^alb:[^\s]+$/i.test(scopeId)) {
  574 |     return null;
  575 |   }
  576 | 
  577 |   return { context, scopeId };
  578 | }

  827 |           message: decision.reason.message,

  829 |         });
  830 |         return;
  831 |       }

  917 |   const cacheAlbumSessionTokens = React.useCallback(
  918 |     (args: {
  919 |       albumId: string;
  920 |       st: string | null;
  921 |       expiresAt: string | number;
  922 |       tracks: AlbumSessionToken[];
  923 |       sampleSession?: {
  924 |         id: string;
  925 |         expiresAt: string | number;
  926 |       } | null;
  927 |     }): boolean => {
  928 |       const expiresAtMs =
  929 |         typeof args.expiresAt === "number"
  930 |           ? args.expiresAt * 1000
  931 |           : Date.parse(String(args.expiresAt));
  932 | 
  933 |       if (!Number.isFinite(expiresAtMs)) return false;
  934 | 
  935 |       const byPlaybackId = new Map<
  936 |         string,
  937 |         { token: string; expiresAtMs: number }
  938 |       >();
  939 | 
  940 |       for (const t of args.tracks) {
  941 |         const playbackId = (t.playbackId ?? "").trim();
  942 |         const token = (t.token ?? "").trim();
  943 | 
  944 |         const trackExpiresAtMs =
  945 |           typeof t.expiresAt === "number"
  946 |             ? t.expiresAt * 1000
  947 |             : Date.parse(String(t.expiresAt));
  948 | 
  949 |         if (!playbackId || !token || !Number.isFinite(trackExpiresAtMs)) {
  950 |           continue;
  951 |         }
  952 | 
  953 |         const entry = { token, expiresAtMs: trackExpiresAtMs };
  954 |         byPlaybackId.set(playbackId, entry);
  955 |         tokenCacheRef.current.set(playbackId, entry);
  956 |       }
  957 | 
  958 |       if (byPlaybackId.size === 0) return false;
  959 | 
  960 |       albumSessionCacheRef.current.set(albumSessionKey(args.albumId, args.st), {
  961 |         albumId: args.albumId,
  962 |         st: args.st,
  963 |         expiresAtMs,
  964 |         byPlaybackId,
  965 |       });
  966 | 
  967 |       if (args.sampleSession) {
  968 |         const sampleExpiresAtMs =
  969 |           typeof args.sampleSession.expiresAt === "number"
  970 |             ? args.sampleSession.expiresAt * 1000
  971 |             : Date.parse(String(args.sampleSession.expiresAt));
  972 | 
  973 |         if (Number.isFinite(sampleExpiresAtMs)) {
  974 |           anonSampleSessionRef.current = {
  975 |             id: args.sampleSession.id,
  976 |             expiresAtMs: sampleExpiresAtMs,
  977 |           };
  978 |         }
  979 |       }
  980 | 
  981 |       return true;
  982 |     },
  983 |     [albumSessionKey],
  984 |   );
  985 | 
  986 |   const getCachedTokenForPlaybackId = React.useCallback(
  987 |     (playbackId: string): { token: string; expiresAtMs: number } | null => {

  989 |       if (direct && Date.now() < direct.expiresAtMs - 5000) return direct;
  990 |       return null;

  995 |   const prefetchAlbumSession = React.useCallback(
  996 |     async (args: {
  997 |       albumId: string | null | undefined;
  998 |       st?: string | null;
  999 |       startPlaybackId?: string | null;
 1000 |       signal?: AbortSignal;
 1001 |       surfaceGate?: boolean;
 1002 |     }): Promise<boolean> => {
 1003 |       const albumId = normalizeAlbumId(args.albumId);
 1004 |       if (!albumId) return false;
 1005 | 
 1006 |       const st = args.st ?? getShareTokenFromLocation();
 1007 |       const startPlaybackId = args.startPlaybackId?.trim() || null;
 1008 | 
 1009 |       const key = albumSessionKey(albumId, st);
 1010 | 
 1011 |       const cached = albumSessionCacheRef.current.get(key);
 1012 |       if (cached && Date.now() < cached.expiresAtMs - 5000) return true;
 1013 | 
 1014 |       const existing = albumSessionInFlightRef.current.get(key);
 1015 |       if (existing) return existing;
 1016 | 
 1017 |       const promise = (async () => {
 1018 |         try {
 1019 |           sendAudioDebug({
 1020 |             event: "album-session-requested",
 1021 |             albumId,
 1022 |             source: "AudioEngine",
 1023 |           });
 1024 | 
 1025 |           const res = await fetch("/api/mux/album-session", {
 1026 |             method: "POST",
 1027 |             headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
 1028 |             body: JSON.stringify({
 1029 |               albumId,
 1030 |               ...(st ? { st } : {}),
 1031 |               ...(startPlaybackId ? { startPlaybackId } : {}),
 1032 |             }),
 1033 |             signal: args.signal,
 1034 |           });
 1035 | 
 1036 |           let data: AlbumSessionResponse | null = null;
 1037 |           try {
 1038 |             data = (await res.json()) as AlbumSessionResponse;
 1039 |           } catch {
 1040 |             data = null;
 1041 |           }
 1042 | 
 1043 |           if (!res.ok || !data || !("ok" in data) || data.ok !== true) {
 1044 |             sendAudioDebug({
 1045 |               event: "album-session-failed",
 1046 |               albumId,
 1047 |               source: "AudioEngine",
 1048 |               detail: `status=${res.status}`,
 1049 |             });
 1050 | 
 1051 |             const gatePayload =
 1052 |               data && "ok" in data && data.ok === false
 1053 |                 ? (data.gate ?? null)
 1054 |                 : null;
 1055 | 
 1056 |             if (args.surfaceGate && gatePayload) {
 1057 |               reportPlaybackGate(
 1058 |                 {
 1059 |                   domain: gatePayload.domain ?? "playback",
 1060 |                   code:
 1061 |                     normalizeGateCodeRaw(gatePayload.code) ??
 1062 |                     "PLAYBACK_CAP_REACHED",
 1063 |                   action: gatePayload.action ?? "login",
 1064 |                   message:
 1065 |                     gatePayload.message ??
 1066 |                     "Enter your email address to continue listening.",
 1067 |                   correlationId:
 1068 |                     gatePayload.correlationId ??
 1069 |                     res.headers.get("x-correlation-id") ??
 1070 |                     null,
 1071 |                   reason: gatePayload.reason,
 1072 |                 },
 1073 |                 res.headers.get("x-correlation-id") ?? null,
 1074 |               );
 1075 |             }
 1076 | 
 1077 |             return false;
 1078 |           }
 1079 | 
 1080 |           sendAudioDebug({
 1081 |             event: "album-session-received",
 1082 |             albumId: data.albumId || albumId,
 1083 |             source: "AudioEngine",
 1084 |             detail: `tracks=${data.tracks.length}`,
 1085 |           });
 1086 | 
 1087 |           return cacheAlbumSessionTokens({
 1088 |             albumId: data.albumId || albumId,
 1089 |             st,
 1090 |             expiresAt: data.expiresAt,
 1091 |             tracks: data.tracks,
 1092 |             sampleSession:
 1093 |               data.mode === "sample" && data.sampleSession
 1094 |                 ? data.sampleSession
 1095 |                 : null,
 1096 |           });
 1097 |         } catch {
 1098 |           return false;
 1099 |         }
 1100 |       })().finally(() => {
 1101 |         albumSessionInFlightRef.current.delete(key);
 1102 |       });
 1103 | 
 1104 |       albumSessionInFlightRef.current.set(key, promise);
 1105 |       return promise;
 1106 |     },
 1107 |     [
 1108 |       albumSessionKey,
 1109 |       cacheAlbumSessionTokens,
 1110 |       getShareTokenFromLocation,
 1111 |       reportPlaybackGate,
 1112 |     ],
 1113 |   );

 1131 |   const fetchSingleToken = React.useCallback(
 1132 |     async (args: {
 1133 |       playbackId: string;
 1134 |       track: PlayerTrack;
 1135 |       signal: AbortSignal;
 1136 |     }): Promise<{ token: string; expiresAtMs: number } | null> => {
 1137 |       const s = pRef.current;
 1138 |       const st = getShareTokenFromLocation();
 1139 | 
 1140 |       const res = await fetch("/api/mux/token", {
 1141 |         method: "POST",
 1142 |         headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
 1143 |         body: JSON.stringify({
 1144 |           playbackId: args.playbackId,
 1145 |           recordingId: args.track.recordingId,
 1146 |           albumId: s.queueContextId,
 1147 |           albumSlug: s.queueContextSlug,
 1148 |           durationMs:
 1149 |             args.track.durationMs ??
 1150 |             s.durationByRecordingId?.[args.track.recordingId],
 1151 |           ...(st ? { st } : {}),
 1152 |         }),
 1153 |         signal: args.signal,
 1154 |       });
 1155 | 
 1156 |       const corr = res.headers.get("x-correlation-id") ?? null;
 1157 | 
 1158 |       let data: TokenResponse | null = null;
 1159 |       try {
 1160 |         data = (await res.json()) as TokenResponse;
 1161 |       } catch {
 1162 |         data = null;
 1163 |       }
 1164 | 
 1165 |       if (!res.ok || !data || !("ok" in data) || data.ok !== true) {
 1166 |         const gatePayloadRaw =
 1167 |           data && "ok" in data && data.ok === false
 1168 |             ? (data.gate ?? null)
 1169 |             : null;
 1170 | 
 1171 |         const msg =
 1172 |           gatePayloadRaw?.message?.trim() ||
 1173 |           (data && "ok" in data && data.ok === false ? data.error : "") ||
 1174 |           `Token error (${res.status})`;
 1175 | 
 1176 |         blockedNonceRef.current.set(args.playbackId, s.reloadNonce);
 1177 |         playIntentRef.current = false;
 1178 | 
 1179 |         if (gatePayloadRaw) {
 1180 |           const rawCode =
 1181 |             normalizeGateCodeRaw(gatePayloadRaw.code) ?? "INVALID_REQUEST";
 1182 |           const action: GateAction = gatePayloadRaw.action ?? "wait";
 1183 |           const payload: GatePayload = {
 1184 |             domain: (gatePayloadRaw.domain ?? "playback") as GateDomain,
 1185 |             code: rawCode,
 1186 |             action,
 1187 |             message: gatePayloadRaw.message ?? msg,
 1188 |             correlationId: gatePayloadRaw.correlationId ?? corr ?? null,
 1189 |             reason: gatePayloadRaw.reason,
 1190 |           };
 1191 |           reportPlaybackGate(payload, corr);
 1192 |         } else {
 1193 |           clearPlaybackGate();
 1194 |         }
 1195 | 
 1196 |         mediaSurface.setStatus("blocked");
 1197 |         return null;
 1198 |       }
 1199 | 
 1200 |       const expiresAtMs =
 1201 |         typeof data.expiresAt === "number"
 1202 |           ? data.expiresAt * 1000
 1203 |           : Date.parse(String(data.expiresAt));
 1204 | 
 1205 |       if (!Number.isFinite(expiresAtMs)) return null;
 1206 | 
 1207 |       const token = { token: data.token, expiresAtMs };
 1208 |       tokenCacheRef.current.set(args.playbackId, token);
 1209 |       blockedNonceRef.current.delete(args.playbackId);
 1210 |       clearPlaybackGate();
 1211 | 
 1212 |       return token;
 1213 |     },
 1214 |     [clearPlaybackGate, getShareTokenFromLocation, reportPlaybackGate],
 1215 |   );

 1217 |   const ensureTokenForTrack = React.useCallback(

 1222 |     }): Promise<{ token: string; expiresAtMs: number } | null> => {

 1267 |   const attachTrackToDeck = React.useCallback(

 1353 |           recordingId,
 1354 |           playbackId,
 1355 |           source: `AudioEngine.${args.deckId}`,
 1356 |         });

 1363 |             JSON.stringify({
 1364 |               readyState: a.readyState,
 1365 |               networkState: a.networkState,
 1366 |               currentTimeSec: Number(a.currentTime.toFixed(3)),
 1367 |               bufferedAheadSec: Number(bufferedAheadSeconds(a).toFixed(3)),
 1368 |             });
 1369 | 
 1370 |           const cleanup = () => {
 1371 |             a.removeEventListener("loadedmetadata", onMaybeReady);
 1372 |             a.removeEventListener("canplay", onMaybeReady);
 1373 |             a.removeEventListener("canplaythrough", onMaybeReady);
 1374 |             a.removeEventListener("progress", onMaybeReady);
 1375 |             a.removeEventListener("error", onError);
 1376 |

 1379 |               timeoutId = null;
 1380 |             }
 1381 |           };

 1384 |             if (settled) return;
 1385 | 
 1386 |             settled = true;
 1387 |             cleanup();
 1388 | 
 1389 |             const meta = metaByDeckRef.current[args.deckId];
 1390 | 
 1391 |             if (ok && meta?.attachKey === attachKey) {
 1392 |               metaByDeckRef.current[args.deckId] = {
 1393 |                 ...meta,
 1394 |                 prepared: true,
 1395 |               };
 1396 |             }
 1397 |

 1399 |           };

 1412 |                 albumId: pRef.current.queueContextId ?? null,
 1413 |                 recordingId,
 1414 |                 playbackId,
 1415 |                 source: `AudioEngine.${args.deckId}`,
 1416 |                 detail: snapshot(),
 1417 |               });
 1418 |             }
 1419 |

 1421 |           };

 1433 |               detail: snapshot(),
 1434 |             });

 1437 |           };

 1440 |           a.addEventListener("canplay", onMaybeReady);
 1441 |           a.addEventListener("canplaythrough", onMaybeReady);
 1442 |           a.addEventListener("progress", onMaybeReady);
 1443 |           a.addEventListener("error", onError);

 1452 |           }
 1453 | 
 1454 |           timeoutId = window.setTimeout(() => {

 1457 |                 args.reason === "standby"

 1460 |               albumId: pRef.current.queueContextId ?? null,
 1461 |               recordingId,
 1462 |               playbackId,

 1479 |           recordingId,
 1480 |           playbackId,
 1481 |           source: `AudioEngine.${args.deckId}`,
 1482 |         });
 1483 | 
 1484 |         return new Promise<boolean>((resolve) => {
 1485 |           let settled = false;
 1486 | 
 1487 |           const cleanup = () => {
 1488 |             a.removeEventListener("loadedmetadata", onReady);
 1489 |             a.removeEventListener("canplay", onReady);
 1490 |             a.removeEventListener("error", onError);
 1491 |           };

 1493 |           const finish = (ok: boolean) => {
 1494 |             if (settled) return;
 1495 |             settled = true;
 1496 |             cleanup();
 1497 |             const meta = metaByDeckRef.current[args.deckId];
 1498 |             if (ok && meta?.attachKey === attachKey) {
 1499 |               metaByDeckRef.current[args.deckId] = {
 1500 |                 ...meta,
 1501 |                 prepared: true,
 1502 |               };
 1503 |             }
 1504 |             resolve(ok);
 1505 |           };
 1506 | 
 1507 |           const onReady = () => finish(true);
 1508 |           const onError = () => finish(false);
 1509 | 
 1510 |           a.addEventListener("loadedmetadata", onReady);
 1511 |           a.addEventListener("canplay", onReady);
 1512 |           a.addEventListener("error", onError);
 1513 | 
 1514 |           try {
 1515 |             a.src = srcUrl;
 1516 |             a.load();
 1517 |           } catch {

 1519 |           }

 1645 |   const playDeck = React.useCallback(
 1646 |     async (deckId: DeckId, reason: "active" | "promote"): Promise<boolean> => {

 1678 |         });

 1680 |         return false;

 1686 |   const playTargetFromUserGesture = React.useCallback(
 1687 |     (detail: TargetedPlayIntentDetail): void => {
 1688 |       if (!isAppleMobileWebKit()) return;
 1689 |       if (engineBlockedRef.current) return;
 1690 |

 2337 |   React.useEffect(() => {
 2338 |     const clearAudioFeatures = () => {
 2339 |       audioSurface.set({
 2340 |         rms: 0,
 2341 |         bass: 0,
 2342 |         mid: 0,
 2343 |         treble: 0,
 2344 |         centroid: 0,
 2345 |         energy: 0,
 2346 |       });
 2347 |     };
 2348 | 
 2349 |     const isActive = p.status === "playing" || p.status === "loading";
 2350 | 
 2351 |     if (!audioAnalysisReady || !isActive) {
 2352 |       clearAudioFeatures();
 2353 |       return;
 2354 |     }
 2355 | 
 2356 |     let raf: number | null = null;
 2357 | 
 2358 |     const tick = () => {
 2359 |       const analyser = analyserRef.current;
 2360 |       const freq = freqDataRef.current;
 2361 |       const time = timeDataRef.current;
 2362 | 
 2363 |       if (!analyser || !freq || !time) {
 2364 |         clearAudioFeatures();
 2365 |         return;
 2366 |       }
 2367 | 
 2368 |       analyser.getByteFrequencyData(freq);
 2369 |       analyser.getByteTimeDomainData(time);
 2370 | 
 2371 |       let sum = 0;
 2372 |       for (let i = 0; i < time.length; i++) {
 2373 |         const v = (time[i]! - 128) / 128;
 2374 |         sum += v * v;
 2375 |       }
 2376 |       const rms = Math.sqrt(sum / time.length);
 2377 | 
 2378 |       const n = freq.length;
 2379 |       const bassEnd = Math.floor(n * 0.08);
 2380 |       const midEnd = Math.floor(n * 0.35);
 2381 | 
 2382 |       let bass = 0;
 2383 |       let mid = 0;
 2384 |       let treble = 0;
 2385 | 
 2386 |       for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
 2387 |         const v = freq[i]! / 255;
 2388 |         if (i < bassEnd) bass += v;
 2389 |         else if (i < midEnd) mid += v;
 2390 |         else treble += v;
 2391 |       }
 2392 | 
 2393 |       bass /= bassEnd || 1;
 2394 |       mid /= midEnd - bassEnd || 1;
 2395 |       treble /= n - midEnd || 1;
 2396 | 
 2397 |       let weighted = 0;
 2398 |       let total = 0;
 2399 | 
 2400 |       for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
 2401 |         const v = freq[i]! / 255;
 2402 |         weighted += i * v;
 2403 |         total += v;
 2404 |       }
 2405 | 
 2406 |       const centroid = total > 0 ? weighted / total / n : 0;
 2407 | 
 2408 |       audioSurface.set({
 2409 |         rms,
 2410 |         bass,
 2411 |         mid,

 2632 |     const reportTelemetryPlay = (params: {
 2633 |       recordingId: string;
 2634 |       playbackId: string;
 2635 |       progressMs: number;
 2636 |       durationMs: number | null;
 2637 |     }) => {
 2638 |       const { recordingId, playbackId, progressMs, durationMs } = params;
 2639 |       if (!recordingId || !playbackId) return;
 2640 | 
 2641 |       const sessionKey = `${recordingId}:${playbackId}`;
 2642 |       const sentKey = `${sessionKey}:play`;
 2643 | 
 2644 |       if (telemetryPlaySentRef.current.has(sentKey)) return;
 2645 | 
 2646 |       const prevProgress =
 2647 |         telemetryPlayLastProgressMsRef.current.get(sessionKey);
 2648 |       telemetryPlayLastProgressMsRef.current.set(sessionKey, progressMs);
 2649 | 
 2650 |       if (prevProgress == null) {
 2651 |         telemetryPlayAccumulatedMsRef.current.set(sessionKey, 0);
 2652 |         return;
 2653 |       }
 2654 | 
 2655 |       const deltaMs = progressMs - prevProgress;
 2656 | 
 2657 |       if (deltaMs <= 0 || deltaMs > 5_000) {
 2658 |         telemetryPlayLastProgressMsRef.current.set(sessionKey, progressMs);
 2659 |         return;
 2660 |       }
 2661 | 
 2662 |       const accumulatedMs =
 2663 |         (telemetryPlayAccumulatedMsRef.current.get(sessionKey) ?? 0) + deltaMs;
 2664 | 
 2665 |       telemetryPlayAccumulatedMsRef.current.set(sessionKey, accumulatedMs);
 2666 | 
 2667 |       if (accumulatedMs < TELEMETRY_PLAY_THRESHOLD_MS) return;
 2668 | 
 2669 |       telemetryPlaySentRef.current.add(sentKey);
 2670 |

 2692 |

 2704 |

 2710 |         event: "complete",

 2970 |     const armEarlyHandoff = (args: {
 2971 |       deckId: DeckId;
 2972 |       currentTrack: PlayerTrack;
 2973 |       nextTrack: PlayerTrack;
 2974 |     }): void => {
 2975 |       if (
 2976 |         GAPLESS_EARLY_PROMOTION_LEAD_MS <= 0 ||
 2977 |         isAppleMobileWebKit() ||
 2978 |         earlyHandoffFrame != null
 2979 |       ) {
 2980 |         return;
 2981 |       }
 2982 | 
 2983 |       const currentPlaybackId = (args.currentTrack.muxPlaybackId ?? "").trim();
 2984 |       const handoffKey = `${args.currentTrack.recordingId}:${
 2985 |         args.nextTrack.recordingId
 2986 |       }:${telemetrySessionIdRef.current ?? currentPlaybackId}`;
 2987 | 
 2988 |       if (earlyHandoffKey === handoffKey) return;
 2989 |       earlyHandoffKey = handoffKey;
 2990 | 
 2991 |       const tick = () => {
 2992 |         earlyHandoffFrame = null;
 2993 | 
 2994 |         const activeAudio = getAudio(args.deckId);
 2995 |         const player = pRef.current;
 2996 |         const currentTrack = player.current;
 2997 |         const playbackId = (currentTrack?.muxPlaybackId ?? "").trim();
 2998 | 
 2999 |         if (
 3000 |           !activeAudio ||
 3001 |           activeDeckRef.current !== args.deckId ||
 3002 |           !currentTrack ||
 3003 |           currentTrack.recordingId !== args.currentTrack.recordingId ||
 3004 |           playbackId !== currentPlaybackId ||
 3005 |           activeAudio.paused ||
 3006 |           activeAudio.ended
 3007 |         ) {
 3008 |           earlyHandoffKey = null;
 3009 |           return;
 3010 |         }
 3011 | 
 3012 |         const durationMs =
 3013 |           readFiniteMediaDurationMs(activeAudio) ||
 3014 |           (playbackId
 3015 |             ? (player.assetDurationByPlaybackId[playbackId] ?? 0)
 3016 |             : 0) ||
 3017 |           player.durationByRecordingId[currentTrack.recordingId] ||
 3018 |           currentTrack.durationMs ||
 3019 |           0;
 3020 | 
 3021 |         if (durationMs <= 0) {
 3022 |           earlyHandoffKey = null;
 3023 |           return;
 3024 |         }
 3025 | 
 3026 |         const remainingMs =
 3027 |           durationMs - Math.floor(Math.max(0, activeAudio.currentTime) * 1000);
 3028 | 
 3029 |         if (remainingMs > GAPLESS_EARLY_PROMOTION_LEAD_MS) {
 3030 |           earlyHandoffFrame = window.requestAnimationFrame(tick);
 3031 |           return;
 3032 |         }
 3033 | 
 3034 |         earlyHandoffKey = null;
 3035 | 
 3036 |         if (remainingMs < 0 || !hasPreparedStandbyForTrack(args.nextTrack)) {
 3037 |           return;
 3038 |         }
 3039 | 
 3040 |         sendAudioDebug({
 3041 |           event: "gapless-early-promotion",
 3042 |           albumId: player.queueContextId ?? null,
 3043 |           recordingId: currentTrack.recordingId,
 3044 |           playbackId,
 3045 |           source: `AudioEngine.${args.deckId}`,
 3046 |           detail: `remaining=${remainingMs};next=${args.nextTrack.recordingId}`,
 3047 |         });

 3049 |         void handleAutoAdvance();
 3050 |       };
 3051 | 
 3052 |       earlyHandoffFrame = window.requestAnimationFrame(tick);
 3053 |     };

 3055 |     const onTime = (deckId: DeckId) => {

 3066 |       const curId = currentTrack?.recordingId ?? "";
 3067 |       const currentPlaybackId = (currentTrack?.muxPlaybackId ?? "").trim();
 3068 | 
 3069 |       const catalogueDurationMs =
 3070 |         (curId ? pRef.current.durationByRecordingId[curId] : 0) ||
 3071 |         currentTrack?.durationMs ||
 3072 |         0;
 3073 | 
 3074 |       const cachedAssetDurationMs = currentPlaybackId
 3075 |         ? (pRef.current.assetDurationByPlaybackId[currentPlaybackId] ?? 0)
 3076 |         : 0;
 3077 | 
 3078 |       // The live element is closest to the actual delivered rendition and is
 3079 |       // therefore authoritative for playback, telemetry, and end detection.
 3080 |       const liveAssetDurationMs = readFiniteMediaDurationMs(a);
 3081 | 
 3082 |       const durMs =
 3083 |         liveAssetDurationMs || cachedAssetDurationMs || catalogueDurationMs;
 3084 | 
 3085 |       if (durMs <= 0) return;
 3086 | 
 3087 |       const heartbeatBucket = Math.floor(ms / 60_000);
 3088 |       const heartbeatKey = `${curId}:${heartbeatBucket}`;
 3089 | 
 3090 |       if (
 3091 |         heartbeatBucket > 0 &&
 3092 |         debugProgressHeartbeatRef.current !== heartbeatKey
 3093 |       ) {
 3094 |         debugProgressHeartbeatRef.current = heartbeatKey;
 3095 |         sendAudioDebug({
 3096 |           event: "playback-progress-heartbeat",
 3097 |           albumId: pRef.current.queueContextId ?? null,
 3098 |           recordingId: curId,
 3099 |           playbackId: pRef.current.current?.muxPlaybackId ?? null,
 3100 |           source: `AudioEngine.${deckId}`,
 3101 |           detail: `progress=${ms};duration=${durMs}`,
 3102 |         });
 3103 |       }
 3104 |

 3106 |         durationSec: durMs / 1000,
 3107 |         positionSec: ms / 1000,
 3108 |         playbackRate: 1,
 3109 |       });
 3110 | 
 3111 |       reportTelemetryPlay({
 3112 |         recordingId: curId,
 3113 |         playbackId: telemetrySessionIdRef.current ?? "",
 3114 |         progressMs: ms,
 3115 |         durationMs: durMs,
 3116 |       });
 3117 | 
 3118 |       reportTelemetryProgress({
 3119 |         recordingId: curId,
 3120 |         playbackId: telemetrySessionIdRef.current ?? "",
 3121 |         progressMs: ms,
 3122 |         durationMs: durMs,
 3123 |       });

 3125 |       const deckMeta = metaByDeckRef.current[deckId];

 3127 |         telemetrySessionIdRef.current ?? ""
 3128 |       }`;
 3129 |

 3131 |         deckMeta?.hlsPath === "static-m4a" &&

 3135 |         staticM4aProgressProofRef.current = staticM4aProgressProofKey;
 3136 |

 3138 |           event: "static-m4a-progress-confirmed",

 3140 |           recordingId: curId,
 3141 |           playbackId: pRef.current.current?.muxPlaybackId ?? null,

 3147 |             bufferedAheadSec: Number(bufferedAheadSeconds(a).toFixed(3)),
 3148 |           }),
 3149 |         });
 3150 |       }

 3154 |

 3156 |         nextTrack &&
 3157 |         remainingMs > 0 &&
 3158 |         remainingMs <= STANDBY_PREPARE_WINDOW_MS

 3161 |           telemetrySessionIdRef.current ?? ""
 3162 |         }`;

 3168 |         }
 3169 |       }

 3171 |       if (

 3175 |         remainingMs <= GAPLESS_EARLY_PROMOTION_ARM_WINDOW_MS

 3177 |         armEarlyHandoff({
 3178 |           deckId,
 3179 |           currentTrack,
 3180 |           nextTrack,
 3181 |         });
 3182 |       }
 3183 | 
 3184 |       const pct = ms / durMs;
 3185 | 
 3186 |       if (pct >= 0.9) {
 3187 |         void reportTelemetryComplete({
 3188 |           recordingId: curId,
 3189 |           playbackId: telemetrySessionIdRef.current ?? "",
 3190 |           progressMs: ms,
 3191 |           durationMs: durMs,
 3192 |         });
 3193 |       }

 3410 |       a.addEventListener("playing", handlers.playing);

 3412 |       a.addEventListener("waiting", handlers.waiting);
 3413 |       a.addEventListener("stalled", handlers.stalled);
 3414 |       a.addEventListener("canplay", handlers.canplay);
 3415 |       a.addEventListener("canplaythrough", handlers.canplaythrough);
 3416 |       a.addEventListener("ended", handlers.ended);
 3417 |       a.addEventListener("error", handlers.error);
 3418 |       a.addEventListener("suspend", handlers.suspend);

  259 | function isStreamingPlatform(v: unknown): v is StreamingPlatform {

  270 | function isPlatformLink(v: unknown): v is PlatformLink {
  271 |   if (typeof v !== "object" || v === null) return false;
  272 | 
  273 |   const maybe = v as Record<string, unknown>;
  274 | 
  275 |   return (
  276 |     isStreamingPlatform(maybe.platform) &&
  277 |     typeof maybe.url === "string" &&
  278 |     /^https:\/\//i.test(maybe.url)
  279 |   );
  280 | }

  376 | 
  377 | function AvailableOnRibbon({
  378 |   links,
  379 | }: Readonly<{ links: readonly PlatformLink[] }>) {
  380 |   if (!links.length) return null;
  381 | 
  382 |   const order: StreamingPlatform[] = [
  383 |     "spotify",
  384 |     "appleMusic",
  385 |     "youtubeMusic",
  386 |     "amazonMusic",
  387 |     "tidal",
  388 |     "deezer",
  389 |   ];
  390 | 
  391 |   const byPlatform = new Map<StreamingPlatform, string>();
  392 |   for (const l of links) byPlatform.set(l.platform, l.url);
  393 | 
  394 |   const ordered = order
  395 |     .map((p) => ({ platform: p, url: byPlatform.get(p) }))
  396 |     .filter((x): x is { platform: StreamingPlatform; url: string } =>
  397 |       Boolean(x.url),
  398 |     );
  399 | 
  400 |   if (!ordered.length) return null;
  401 | 
  402 |   return (
  403 |     <div className="afAvailWrap" aria-label="Available on">
  404 |       <div className="afAvailLine" aria-hidden="true" />
  405 |       <div className="afAvailLabel">AVAILABLE ON</div>
  406 | 
  407 |       <div className="afAvailIcons">
  408 |         {ordered.map(({ platform, url }) => (
  409 |           <a
  410 |             key={platform}
  411 |             href={url}
  412 |             target="_blank"
  413 |             rel="noopener noreferrer external nofollow"
  414 |             className="afAvailIcon"
  415 |             aria-label={`Open on ${platform}`}
  416 |             title={`Open on ${platform}`}
  417 |           >
  418 |             <PlatformIcon platform={platform} />
  419 |           </a>
  420 |         ))}
  421 |       </div>
  422 |     </div>
  423 |   );
  424 | }

  426 | type PlayerApi = ReturnType<typeof usePlayer>;
  427 | 
  428 | type FullPlayerProps = Readonly<{
  429 |   bundle: AlbumPlayerBundle;
  430 |   albums: AlbumNavItem[];
  431 |   onSelectAlbum?: (slug: string) => void;
  432 |   isBrowsingAlbum?: boolean;
  433 |   tier?: Tier;

  436 | type EffectivePlayerView = Readonly<{
  437 |   albumSlug: string;
  438 |   album: AlbumPlayerBundle["album"];
  439 |   tracks: PlayerTrack[];
  440 | }>;
  441 | 
  442 | function resolveAccessIdentityKey(
  443 |   params: Readonly<{
  444 |     clerkAuthLoaded: boolean;
  445 |     isSignedIn: boolean | undefined;
  446 |     userId: string | null | undefined;
  447 |     sessionId: string | null | undefined;
  448 |   }>,
  449 | ): string {
  450 |   if (!params.clerkAuthLoaded) return "clerk:loading";
  451 |   if (!params.isSignedIn) return "clerk:anonymous";
  452 | 
  453 |   return `clerk:user:${params.userId ?? ""}:session:${params.sessionId ?? ""}`;
  454 | }
  455 | 
  456 | function resolveEffectivePlayerView(
  457 |   params: Readonly<{
  458 |     bundle: AlbumPlayerBundle;
  459 |     stableView: StableView | null;
  460 |     isBrowsingAlbum: boolean;
  461 |   }>,
  462 | ): EffectivePlayerView {
  463 |   const { bundle, stableView, isBrowsingAlbum } = params;
  464 |   const shouldUseStableView = Boolean(
  465 |     isBrowsingAlbum &&
  466 |     stableView?.album &&
  467 |     (!bundle.album || bundle.tracks.length === 0),
  468 |   );
  469 | 
  470 |   if (shouldUseStableView && stableView) {
  471 |     return {
  472 |       albumSlug: stableView.albumSlug,
  473 |       album: stableView.album,
  474 |       tracks: stableView.tracks,
  475 |     };
  476 |   }
  477 | 
  478 |   return {
  479 |     albumSlug: bundle.albumSlug,
  480 |     album: bundle.album,
  481 |     tracks: bundle.tracks,
  482 |   };
  483 | }
  484 | 
  485 | function accessStatesEqual(
  486 |   left: AccessState | null,
  487 |   right: AccessState | null,
  488 | ): boolean {
  489 |   if (!left || !right) return left === right;
  490 |   return JSON.stringify(left) === JSON.stringify(right);
  491 | }
  492 | 
  493 | function mergeAccessState(
  494 |   previous: AccessState | null,
  495 |   next: AccessState | null,
  496 | ): AccessState | null {
  497 |   return accessStatesEqual(previous, next) ? previous : next;
  498 | }
  499 | 
  500 | function isAuthOrLegacyCapCode(code: string): boolean {
  501 |   return (
  502 |     code === "AUTH_REQUIRED" ||
  503 |     code === "CAP_REACHED" ||
  504 |     code === "ANON_CAP_REACHED"
  505 |   );
  506 | }
  507 | 
  508 | function hasPlaybackIntentForAlbum(
  509 |   player: PlayerApi,
  510 |   pendingInAlbum: boolean,
  511 | ): boolean {
  512 |   return (
  513 |     player.playRequestPending ||
  514 |     player.intent === "play" ||
  515 |     player.status === "loading" ||
  516 |     player.status === "playing" ||
  517 |     player.status === "paused" ||
  518 |     pendingInAlbum
  519 |   );
  520 | }
  521 | 
  522 | function reconcilePlayerWithAccessDecision(
  523 |   params: Readonly<{
  524 |     player: PlayerApi;
  525 |     decision: AccessState;
  526 |     recordingIds: ReadonlySet<string>;
  527 |     albumKey: string | null;
  528 |   }>,
  529 | ): void {
  530 |   const { player, decision, recordingIds, albumKey } = params;
  531 | 
  532 |   if (decision.allowed) {
  533 |     if (player.lastError) player.clearError();
  534 |     return;
  535 |   }
  536 | 
  537 |   const currentInAlbum = Boolean(
  538 |     player.current?.recordingId && recordingIds.has(player.current.recordingId),
  539 |   );
  540 |   const pendingInAlbum = Boolean(
  541 |     player.pendingRecordingId && recordingIds.has(player.pendingRecordingId),
  542 |   );
  543 |   const queueIsAlbum = Boolean(albumKey && player.queueContextId === albumKey);
  544 |   const hasIntent = hasPlaybackIntentForAlbum(player, pendingInAlbum);
  545 |   const shouldBlockAlbum =
  546 |     currentInAlbum || pendingInAlbum || (queueIsAlbum && hasIntent);
  547 | 
  548 |   const code = typeof decision.code === "string" ? decision.code.trim() : "";
  549 |   if (!shouldBlockAlbum || (isAuthOrLegacyCapCode(code) && !hasIntent)) return;
  550 | 
  551 |   try {
  552 |     player.pause();
  553 |   } catch {
  554 |     // ignore
  555 |   }
  556 | 
  557 |   if (player.status === "loading") {
  558 |     player.setStatusExternal("idle");
  559 |   }
  560 | }
  561 | 
  562 | function accessFailureFallback(catalogueId: string): AccessState {
  563 |   return {
  564 |     forCatalogueId: catalogueId,
  565 |     allowed: true,
  566 |     embargoed: false,
  567 |     releaseAt: null,
  568 |     code: "ACCESS_CHECK_ERROR",
  569 |     reason: "Access check failed (client).",
  570 |     corr: null,
  571 |     shareTokenAccess: null,
  572 |     sharePlaybackContext: null,
  573 |     sharePlaybackScopeId: null,
  574 |   };
  575 | }
  576 | 
  577 | function resolveAccessForAlbum(
  578 |   catalogueId: string | null,
  579 |   access: AccessState | null,
  580 | ): AccessState | null {
  581 |   if (!catalogueId) return null;
  582 |   if (access?.forCatalogueId !== catalogueId) return null;
  583 |   return access;
  584 | }
  585 | 
  586 | function isPlayerPlayingish(player: PlayerApi): boolean {
  587 |   return (
  588 |     player.status === "playing" ||
  589 |     player.status === "loading" ||
  590 |     player.playRequestPending
  591 |   );
  592 | }
  593 | 
  594 | type PlaybackAccessUiState = Readonly<{
  595 |   accessKnown: boolean;
  596 |   isPlaybackGate: boolean;
  597 |   canPlay: boolean;
  598 |   canAttemptPlay: boolean;
  599 | }>;
  600 | 
  601 | function derivePlaybackAccessUiState(
  602 |   tracks: readonly PlayerTrack[],
  603 |   accessForAlbum: AccessState | null,
  604 | ): PlaybackAccessUiState {
  605 |   const accessKnown = accessForAlbum !== null;
  606 |   const hasPlayableTracks = tracks.length > 0;
  607 |   const isPlaybackGate = Boolean(
  608 |     hasPlayableTracks &&
  609 |     accessKnown &&
  610 |     accessForAlbum?.allowed === false &&
  611 |     isPlaybackGateCode(accessForAlbum.code),
  612 |   );
  613 |   const canPlay = Boolean(
  614 |     hasPlayableTracks && accessKnown && accessForAlbum?.allowed !== false,
  615 |   );
  616 | 
  617 |   return {
  618 |     accessKnown,
  619 |     isPlaybackGate,
  620 |     canPlay,
  621 |     canAttemptPlay: canPlay || isPlaybackGate,
  622 |   };
  623 | }
  624 | 
  625 | type EmbargoUiState = Readonly<{
  626 |   releaseAtMs: number;
  627 |   showEmbargo: boolean;
  628 |   showShareTokenEmbargoAccess: boolean;
  629 | }>;
  630 | 
  631 | function deriveEmbargoUiState(
  632 |   params: Readonly<{
  633 |     embargo: NonNullable<AlbumPlayerBundle["album"]>["embargo"];
  634 |     accessForAlbum: AccessState | null;
  635 |     shareToken: string | null;
  636 |   }>,
  637 | ): EmbargoUiState {
  638 |   const releaseAtMs = params.embargo?.releaseAt
  639 |     ? Date.parse(params.embargo.releaseAt)
  640 |     : Number.NaN;
  641 |   const accessResolved = params.accessForAlbum !== null;
  642 |   const accessAllowsEmbargoBypass = params.accessForAlbum?.allowed === true;
  643 |   const showEmbargo = Boolean(
  644 |     params.embargo?.embargoed &&
  645 |     Number.isFinite(releaseAtMs) &&
  646 |     !accessAllowsEmbargoBypass &&
  647 |     (!params.shareToken || accessResolved),
  648 |   );
  649 |   const showShareTokenEmbargoAccess = Boolean(
  650 |     params.embargo?.embargoed &&
  651 |     params.accessForAlbum?.allowed === true &&
  652 |     params.accessForAlbum.shareTokenAccess,
  653 |   );
  654 | 
  655 |   return { releaseAtMs, showEmbargo, showShareTokenEmbargoAccess };
  656 | }
  657 | 
  658 | function isAlbumPlaybackActive(
  659 |   albumKey: string | null,
  660 |   queueContextId: string | undefined,
  661 | ): boolean {
  662 |   return Boolean(albumKey && queueContextId === albumKey);
  663 | }
  664 | 
  665 | type AlbumTransportState = Readonly<{
  666 |   albumIdx: number;
  667 |   prevDisabled: boolean;
  668 |   nextDisabled: boolean;
  669 | }>;
  670 | 
  671 | function deriveAlbumTransportState(
  672 |   params: Readonly<{
  673 |     currentRecordingId: string;
  674 |     tracks: readonly PlayerTrack[];
  675 |     transportLock: boolean;
  676 |   }>,
  677 | ): AlbumTransportState {
  678 |   const albumIdx = params.currentRecordingId
  679 |     ? params.tracks.findIndex(
  680 |         (track) => track.recordingId === params.currentRecordingId,
  681 |       )
  682 |     : -1;
  683 | 
  684 |   if (albumIdx < 0) {
  685 |     return { albumIdx, prevDisabled: true, nextDisabled: true };
  686 |   }
  687 | 
  688 |   return {
  689 |     albumIdx,
  690 |     prevDisabled: params.transportLock || albumIdx === 0,
  691 |     nextDisabled: params.transportLock || albumIdx === params.tracks.length - 1,
  692 |   };
  693 | }
  694 | 
  695 | function isCoarsePointerDevice(): boolean {
  696 |   if (typeof window === "undefined") return false;
  697 | 
  698 |   try {
  699 |     return window.matchMedia?.("(pointer: coarse)").matches ?? false;
  700 |   } catch {
  701 |     return "ontouchstart" in window;
  702 |   }
  703 | }
  704 | 
  705 | function trackRowColors(
  706 |   canPlay: boolean,
  707 |   isCurrent: boolean,
  708 | ): Readonly<{ titleColor: string; subColor: string }> {
  709 |   if (!canPlay) {
  710 |     return {
  711 |       titleColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.38)",
  712 |       subColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.32)",
  713 |     };
  714 |   }
  715 | 
  716 |   if (isCurrent) {
  717 |     return {
  718 |       titleColor: "var(--accent)",
  719 |       subColor: "color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 70%, rgba(255,255,255,0.70))",
  720 |     };
  721 |   }
  722 | 
  723 |   return {
  724 |     titleColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.92)",
  725 |     subColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.70)",
  726 |   };
  727 | }
  728 | 
  729 | function trackRowRadius(index: number, trackCount: number): string {
  730 |   if (index === 0) return "14px 14px 0 0";
  731 |   if (index === trackCount - 1) return "0 0 14px 14px";
  732 |   return "0";
  733 | }
  734 | 
  735 | type TrackRowView = Readonly<{
  736 |   isCurrent: boolean;
  737 |   shimmerTitle: boolean;
  738 |   isNowPlaying: boolean;
  739 |   titleColor: string;
  740 |   subColor: string;
  741 |   restingBackground: string;
  742 |   rowRadius: string;
  743 |   dataReason: string;
  744 |   cursor: "pointer" | "default";
  745 |   opacity: number;
  746 | }>;
  747 | 
  748 | function deriveTrackRowView(
  749 |   params: Readonly<{
  750 |     track: PlayerTrack;
  751 |     index: number;
  752 |     trackCount: number;
  753 |     currentRecordingId: string | undefined;
  754 |     pendingRecordingId: string | undefined;
  755 |     playerStatus: PlayerApi["status"];
  756 |     playerIntent: PlayerApi["intent"];
  757 |     loadingReason: PlayerApi["loadingReason"];
  758 |     canPlay: boolean;
  759 |     canAttemptPlay: boolean;
  760 |     isSelected: boolean;
  761 |   }>,
  762 | ): TrackRowView {
  763 |   const isCurrent = params.currentRecordingId === params.track.recordingId;
  764 |   const isPending = params.pendingRecordingId === params.track.recordingId;
  765 |   const shimmerTitle =
  766 |     params.playerStatus === "loading" && isPending && !isCurrent;
  767 |   const isNowPlaying = Boolean(
  768 |     isCurrent &&
  769 |     (params.playerStatus === "playing" ||
  770 |       params.playerStatus === "loading" ||
  771 |       params.playerIntent === "play"),
  772 |   );
  773 |   const { titleColor, subColor } = trackRowColors(params.canPlay, isCurrent);
  774 |   const baseBackground = params.isSelected
  775 |     ? "rgba(255,255,255,0.14)"
  776 |     : "transparent";
  777 |   const restingBackground =
  778 |     isCurrent && !params.isSelected ? "transparent" : baseBackground;
  779 |   const dataReason =
  780 |     isCurrent && params.playerStatus === "loading"
  781 |       ? (params.loadingReason ?? "")
  782 |       : "";
  783 | 
  784 |   return {
  785 |     isCurrent,
  786 |     shimmerTitle,
  787 |     isNowPlaying,
  788 |     titleColor,
  789 |     subColor,
  790 |     restingBackground,
  791 |     rowRadius: trackRowRadius(params.index, params.trackCount),
  792 |     dataReason,
  793 |     cursor: params.canAttemptPlay ? "pointer" : "default",
  794 |     opacity: params.canPlay ? 1 : 0.75,
  795 |   };
  796 | }
  797 | 
  798 | function trackPlayLabel(track: PlayerTrack, index: number): string {
  799 |   const title = track.title?.trim();
  800 |   const subject = title || `track ${index + 1}`;
  801 |   return `Play ${subject}`;
  802 | }

  810 |   const subject = title || `track ${index + 1}`;

  854 | function EmbargoNotices(
  855 |   props: Readonly<{
  856 |     showEmbargo: boolean;

  982 | function TransportControls(
  983 |   props: Readonly<{
  984 |     prevDisabled: boolean;
  985 |     nextDisabled: boolean;
  986 |     canAttemptPlay: boolean;
  987 |     canPlay: boolean;
  988 |     playLock: boolean;
  989 |     playingThisAlbum: boolean;
  990 |     onDownload: () => void;
  991 |     onPrevious: () => void;
  992 |     onTogglePlay: () => void;
  993 |     onNext: () => void;
  994 |     onShare: () => void;
  995 |   }>,
  996 | ) {
  997 |   const playLabel = props.playingThisAlbum ? "Pause" : "Play";
  998 |   const playClick =
  999 |     props.canAttemptPlay && !props.playLock ? props.onTogglePlay : undefined;
 1000 |   const playCursor = props.canAttemptPlay ? "pointer" : "default";
 1001 |   const playOpacity = props.canAttemptPlay ? 1 : 0.55;
 1002 |   const playShadow = props.playingThisAlbum
 1003 |     ? "0 18px 50px rgba(0,0,0,0.35)"
 1004 |     : "0 18px 50px rgba(0,0,0,0.30)";
 1005 | 
 1006 |   return (
 1007 |     <div className="afTransportRow">
 1008 |       <div className="afTransportBadgeSlot" aria-hidden="true" />
 1009 | 
 1010 |       <div className="afTransportControls">
 1011 |         <IconCircleBtn label="Download" onClick={props.onDownload}>
 1012 |           <DownloadIcon />
 1013 |         </IconCircleBtn>
 1014 | 
 1015 |         <IconCircleBtn
 1016 |           label="Previous"
 1017 |           disabled={props.prevDisabled}
 1018 |           onClick={props.onPrevious}
 1019 |         >
 1020 |           <PrevIcon />
 1021 |         </IconCircleBtn>
 1022 | 
 1023 |         <div style={{ position: "relative", width: 64, height: 64 }}>
 1024 |           <button
 1025 |             type="button"
 1026 |             onClick={playClick}
 1027 |             disabled={!props.canAttemptPlay || props.playLock}
 1028 |             aria-label={playLabel}
 1029 |             title={playLabel}
 1030 |             style={{
 1031 |               width: 64,
 1032 |               height: 64,
 1033 |               borderRadius: 999,
 1034 |               border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.12)",
 1035 |               background: "rgba(245,245,245,0.95)",
 1036 |               color: "rgba(0,0,0,0.92)",
 1037 |               display: "grid",
 1038 |               placeItems: "center",
 1039 |               cursor: playCursor,
 1040 |               opacity: playOpacity,
 1041 |               boxShadow: playShadow,
 1042 |               transform: "translateZ(0)",
 1043 |               position: "relative",
 1044 |               zIndex: 2,
 1045 |             }}
 1046 |           >
 1047 |             <PlayPauseBig playing={props.playingThisAlbum} />
 1048 |           </button>
 1049 | 
 1050 |           {props.canPlay ? (
 1051 |             <div
 1052 |               style={{
 1053 |                 position: "absolute",
 1054 |                 inset: -5,
 1055 |                 zIndex: 1,
 1056 |                 pointerEvents: "none",
 1057 |                 overflow: "visible",
 1058 |                 isolation: "isolate",
 1059 |                 transform: "translateZ(0)",
 1060 |                 display: "grid",
 1061 |                 placeItems: "center",
 1062 |               }}
 1063 |             >
 1064 |               <PatternRingGlow
 1065 |                 size={64}
 1066 |                 ringPx={1}
 1067 |                 glowPx={2}
 1068 |                 blurPx={4}
 1069 |                 opacity={0.45}
 1070 |                 seed={913}
 1071 |               />
 1072 |             </div>
 1073 |           ) : null}
 1074 |         </div>
 1075 | 
 1076 |         <IconCircleBtn
 1077 |           label="Next"
 1078 |           disabled={props.nextDisabled}
 1079 |           onClick={props.onNext}
 1080 |         >
 1081 |           <NextIcon />
 1082 |         </IconCircleBtn>
 1083 | 
 1084 |         <IconCircleBtn label="Share" onClick={props.onShare}>
 1085 |           <ShareIcon />
 1086 |         </IconCircleBtn>
 1087 |       </div>
 1088 | 
 1089 |       <div aria-hidden="true" />
 1090 |     </div>
 1091 |   );
 1092 | }
