 1094 | function TrackIndexCell(
 1095 |   props: Readonly<{
 1096 |     index: number;
 1097 |     track: PlayerTrack;
 1098 |     isNowPlaying: boolean;
 1099 |     canAttemptPlay: boolean;
 1100 |     isCurrent: boolean;
 1101 |     subColor: string;
 1102 |     onInlinePlay: (track: PlayerTrack) => void;
 1103 |   }>,
 1104 | ) {
 1105 |   let content: React.ReactNode = (
 1106 |     <span className="afTrackIndexNumber">{props.index + 1}</span>
 1107 |   );
 1108 | 
 1109 |   if (props.isNowPlaying) {
 1110 |     content = <NowPlayingPip />;
 1111 |   } else if (props.canAttemptPlay && !props.isCurrent) {
 1112 |     content = (
 1113 |       <span className="afTrackIndexSwap">
 1114 |         <span className="afTrackIndexNumber">{props.index + 1}</span>
 1115 |         <button
 1116 |           type="button"
 1117 |           className="afTrackInlinePlay"
 1118 |           aria-label={trackPlayLabel(props.track, props.index)}
 1119 |           title="Play"
 1120 |           onClick={(event) => {
 1121 |             event.preventDefault();
 1122 |             event.stopPropagation();
 1123 |             props.onInlinePlay(props.track);
 1124 |           }}
 1125 |         >
 1126 |           <TrackInlinePlayIcon />
 1127 |         </button>
 1128 |       </span>
 1129 |     );
 1130 |   }
 1131 | 
 1132 |   return (
 1133 |     <div
 1134 |       style={{
 1135 |         fontSize: 12,
 1136 |         opacity: 0.9,
 1137 |         display: "flex",
 1138 |         alignItems: "center",
 1139 |         gap: 6,
 1140 |         color: props.subColor,
 1141 |         paddingLeft: 12,
 1142 |         justifyContent: "flex-start",
 1143 |         position: "relative",
 1144 |         zIndex: 1,
 1145 |         pointerEvents: "none",
 1146 |       }}
 1147 |     >
 1148 |       {content}
 1149 |     </div>
 1150 |   );
 1151 | }
 1152 | 
 1153 | function parsePublicAlbumPath(pathname: string | null): {
 1154 |   albumSlug: string | null;
 1155 |   displayId: string | null;
 1156 | } {
 1157 |   const p = (pathname ?? "").trim();
 1158 | 
 1159 |   // canonical music surfaces:
 1160 |   // /:slug
 1161 |   // /:slug/:displayId
 1162 |   //
 1163 |   // exclude obvious non-music reserved roots
 1164 |   const reserved = new Set([
 1165 |     "",
 1166 |     "player",
 1167 |     "journal",
 1168 |     "portal",
 1169 |     "posts",
 1170 |     "extras",
 1171 |     "download",
 1172 |     "gift",
 1173 |     "unsubscribe",
 1174 |     "studio",
 1175 |     "admin",
 1176 |     "api",
 1177 |   ]);
 1178 | 
 1179 |   const parts = p
 1180 |     .split("?")[0]
 1181 |     .split("#")[0]
 1182 |     .split("/")
 1183 |     .filter(Boolean)
 1184 |     .map((x) => decodeURIComponent(x).trim())
 1185 |     .filter(Boolean);
 1186 | 
 1187 |   if (parts.length === 0) return { albumSlug: null, displayId: null };
 1188 |   if (parts.length > 2) return { albumSlug: null, displayId: null };
 1189 | 
 1190 |   const albumSlug = parts[0] ?? null;
 1191 |   if (!albumSlug || reserved.has(albumSlug)) {
 1192 |     return { albumSlug: null, displayId: null };
 1193 |   }
 1194 | 
 1195 |   const displayId = parts[1] ?? null;
 1196 |   return { albumSlug, displayId };
 1197 | }
 1198 | 
 1199 | function canonicalCarryQuery(): string {
 1200 |   // Only carry the params that are allowed to exist on canonical public routes.
 1201 |   if (typeof window === "undefined") return "";
 1202 |   const sp = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
 1203 |   const out = new URLSearchParams();
 1204 | 
 1205 |   const st = (sp.get("st") ?? "").trim();
 1206 |   const share = (sp.get("share") ?? "").trim();
 1207 |   const autoplay = (sp.get("autoplay") ?? "").trim();
 1208 | 
 1209 |   if (st) out.set("st", st);
 1210 |   else if (share) out.set("share", share);
 1211 | 
 1212 |   if (autoplay) out.set("autoplay", autoplay);
 1213 | 
 1214 |   // keep utm_* if present
 1215 |   for (const [k, v] of sp.entries()) {
 1216 |     if (k.startsWith("utm_") && v.trim()) out.set(k, v.trim());
 1217 |   }
 1218 | 
 1219 |   const q = out.toString();
 1220 |   return q ? `?${q}` : "";
 1221 | }

 1314 |   const scrollMobilePlayerToTop = React.useCallback(() => {
 1315 |     if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
 1316 |     if (!window.matchMedia("(max-width: 640px)").matches) return;
 1317 | 
 1318 |     const target = fullPlayerTopRef.current;
 1319 |     if (!target) return;

 1321 |     let ancestor: HTMLElement | null = target.parentElement;
 1322 |

 1324 |       const overflowY = window.getComputedStyle(ancestor).overflowY;
 1325 |       const canScroll =
 1326 |         (overflowY === "auto" ||
 1327 |           overflowY === "scroll" ||
 1328 |           overflowY === "overlay") &&
 1329 |         ancestor.scrollHeight > ancestor.clientHeight;
 1330 | 
 1331 |       if (canScroll) {
 1332 |         const ancestorTop = ancestor.getBoundingClientRect().top;
 1333 |         const targetTop = target.getBoundingClientRect().top;
 1334 |         const nextTop = Math.max(
 1335 |           0,
 1336 |           ancestor.scrollTop + targetTop - ancestorTop,
 1337 |         );
 1338 |

 1340 |           top: nextTop,
 1341 |           behavior: "auto",
 1342 |         });
 1343 | 
 1344 |         return;
 1345 |       }
 1346 | 
 1347 |       ancestor = ancestor.parentElement;
 1348 |     }

 1650 |     track: PlayerTrack,

 1652 |       ensureQueue: boolean;

 1654 |     }>,

 1663 |   const onTogglePlay = () => {

 1667 |       openPlaybackGate();
 1668 |       return;
 1669 |     }
 1670 |     if (!canPlay) return;
 1671 | 
 1672 |     const currentRecordingId = p.current?.recordingId ?? "";

 1675 |         null)

 1677 |     const routeTargetsThisAlbum =

 1679 |     const directRouteTrack = routeTargetsThisAlbum
 1680 |       ? (effTracks.find((track) => track.displayId === route.displayId) ?? null)
 1681 |       : null;

 1683 |       directRouteTrack &&
 1684 |       directRouteTrack.recordingId !== currentTrackInThisAlbum?.recordingId,
 1685 |     );
 1686 | 
 1687 |     // A generic album page controls its existing same-album transport natively.
 1688 |     // A direct track URL is more specific: it deliberately selects that track.
 1689 |     if (playingThisAlbum && !directRouteTargetsAnotherTrack) {
 1690 |       window.dispatchEvent(new Event("af:pause-intent"));
 1691 |       p.pause();
 1692 |       return;
 1693 |     }
 1694 | 
 1695 |     if (directRouteTrack && directRouteTargetsAnotherTrack) {
 1696 |       playTrackWithIntent(directRouteTrack, {
 1697 |         ensureQueue: true,
 1698 |         navigationMode: "replace",
 1699 |       });
 1700 |       return;
 1701 |     }
 1702 | 
 1703 |     // Same active album, no differently targeted direct-track URL:
 1704 |     // retain position and resume rather than resetting to track one.
 1705 |     if (currentTrackInThisAlbum) {
 1706 |       playTrackWithIntent(currentTrackInThisAlbum, {
 1707 |         ensureQueue: false,
 1708 |         navigationMode: "replace",
 1709 |       });
 1710 |       return;
 1711 |     }
 1712 | 
 1713 |     // A different album, or an album with no recoverable current track:
 1714 |     // intentionally establish its queue and begin at its first track.
 1715 |     const firstTrack = effTracks[0];
 1716 |     if (!firstTrack) return;
 1717 | 
 1718 |     playTrackWithIntent(firstTrack, {
 1719 |       ensureQueue: true,
 1720 |       navigationMode: "replace",
 1721 |     });
 1722 |   };
 1723 | 
 1724 |   const getDurMs = (t: PlayerTrack) =>
 1725 |     p.durationByRecordingId?.[t.recordingId] ?? t.durationMs;
 1726 |   const renderDur = (t: PlayerTrack) => {
 1727 |     const ms = getDurMs(t) ?? 0;
 1728 |     return ms > 0 ? fmtTime(ms) : "—";
 1729 |   };

 1732 |     albumSlug: effAlbumSlug,

 1735 |     albumId: albumKey ?? undefined,
 1736 |   });

 1739 |     string | null
 1740 |   >(null);
 1741 | 
 1742 |   const isCoarsePointer = isCoarsePointerDevice();
 1743 | 
 1744 |   // --- Album-local transport (FullPlayer) ---
 1745 |   // Only operate within effTracks. Never call p.prev()/p.next() here.
 1746 |

 1749 |     currentRecordingId: curId,

 1754 |   function ensureAlbumQueue() {
 1755 |     // Explicit nulls prevent an earlier private-link attribution context from
 1756 |     // surviving when ordinary album playback replaces the queue.
 1757 |     p.setQueue(effTracks, {
 1758 |       contextId: albumKey ?? undefined,
 1759 |       artworkUrl: effAlbum?.artworkUrl ?? null,
 1760 |       contextSlug: effAlbumSlug,
 1761 |       contextTitle: effAlbum?.title ?? undefined,
 1762 |       contextArtist: effAlbum?.artist ?? undefined,
 1763 |       sharePlaybackContext: queueSharePlaybackContext,
 1764 |       sharePlaybackScopeId: queueSharePlaybackScopeId,
 1765 |     });
 1766 |   }
 1767 | 
 1768 |   const preparedInitialQueueRef = React.useRef<string | null>(null);
 1769 | 
 1770 |   React.useEffect(() => {
 1771 |     // Wait for the access check so a private-link queue receives its resolved
 1772 |     // attribution context before any listener can press play.
 1773 |     if (!accessKnown) return;
 1774 | 
 1775 |     if (!isPublicAlbumRoute || route.albumSlug !== effAlbumSlug || !albumKey) {
 1776 |       return;
 1777 |     }
 1778 | 
 1779 |     const directTrack = route.displayId
 1780 |       ? (effTracks.find((track) => track.displayId === route.displayId) ?? null)
 1781 |       : null;
 1782 | 
 1783 |     const initialTrack = directTrack ?? effTracks[0] ?? null;
 1784 | 
 1785 |     if (!initialTrack) return;
 1786 | 
 1787 |     // Prepare a fresh player around the route's target track—or track one on a
 1788 |     // generic album route—without beginning playback. Never overwrite an
 1789 |     // existing session, including a paused or playing different album.
 1790 |     const hasExistingPlayerSession = Boolean(
 1791 |       p.current || p.queue.length > 0 || p.pendingRecordingId,
 1792 |     );
 1793 | 
 1794 |     if (hasExistingPlayerSession) return;
 1795 | 
 1796 |     const queueKey = `${albumKey}:${initialTrack.recordingId}`;
 1797 | 
 1798 |     if (preparedInitialQueueRef.current === queueKey) return;
 1799 | 
 1800 |     preparedInitialQueueRef.current = queueKey;
 1801 | 
 1802 |     p.setQueue(effTracks, {
 1803 |       contextId: albumKey,
 1804 |       artworkUrl: effAlbum?.artworkUrl ?? null,
 1805 |       contextSlug: effAlbumSlug,
 1806 |       contextTitle: effAlbum?.title ?? undefined,
 1807 |       contextArtist: effAlbum?.artist ?? undefined,
 1808 |       sharePlaybackContext: queueSharePlaybackContext,
 1809 |       sharePlaybackScopeId: queueSharePlaybackScopeId,
 1810 |       initialCurrentRecordingId: initialTrack.recordingId,
 1811 |     });
 1812 |   }, [
 1813 |     accessKnown,
 1814 |     albumKey,
 1815 |     effAlbum?.artworkUrl,
 1816 |     effAlbum?.artist,
 1817 |     effAlbum?.title,
 1818 |     effAlbumSlug,
 1819 |     effTracks,
 1820 |     isPublicAlbumRoute,
 1821 |     p,
 1822 |     queueSharePlaybackContext,
 1823 |     queueSharePlaybackScopeId,
 1824 |     route.albumSlug,
 1825 |     route.displayId,
 1826 |   ]);
 1827 | 
 1828 |   function playAlbumIndex(i: number) {
 1829 |     const t = effTracks[i];
 1830 |     if (!t) return;
 1831 | 
 1832 |     prefetchAlbumSession(t);
 1833 |     ensureAlbumQueue();
 1834 |     if (isPublicAlbumRoute) {
 1835 |       // User-initiated navigation; preserve history.
 1836 |       goCanonicalTrack(t.displayId, "push");
 1837 |     }
 1838 | 
 1839 |     p.play(t);
 1840 |     window.dispatchEvent(new Event("af:play-intent"));
 1841 |   }

 1847 |   const activateTrackRow = (track: PlayerTrack) => {
 1848 |     if (isPlaybackGate) {
 1849 |       openPlaybackGate();
 1850 |       return;
 1851 |     }

 1856 | 
 1857 |     if (isCoarsePointer) {
 1858 |       prefetchAlbumSession(track);
 1859 |       p.play(track);
 1860 |       window.dispatchEvent(new Event("af:play-intent"));
 1861 |       return;
 1862 |     }
 1863 | 
 1864 |     setSelectedRecordingId(track.recordingId);
 1865 |   };
 1866 |

 1868 |     if (isCoarsePointer) return;

 1871 |       return;
 1872 |     }

   13 | function clamp(n: number, min: number, max: number) {
   14 |   return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, n));
   15 | }
   16 | 
   17 | function fmtTimeSec(sec: number) {
   18 |   const s = Math.max(0, Math.floor(sec));
   19 |   const m = Math.floor(s / 60);
   20 |   const r = s % 60;
   21 |   return `${m}:${String(r).padStart(2, "0")}`;
   22 | }

  186 | /** Local share UX */
  187 | function useShareUX() {
  188 |   const shareTarget = React.useCallback(async (target: ShareTarget) => {
  189 |     return performShare(target);
  190 |   }, []);

  195 | function findTrackById(
  196 |   queue: PlayerTrack[],
  197 |   id?: string | null,
  198 | ): PlayerTrack | null {
  199 |   if (!id) return null;
  200 |   return queue.find((t) => t.recordingId === id) ?? null;
  201 | }
  202 | 
  203 | export default function MiniPlayer(props: {
  204 |   onExpand?: () => void;
  205 |   artworkUrl?: string | null;
  206 | }) {
  207 |   const { onExpand, artworkUrl = null } = props;
  208 |   const p = usePlayer();
  209 |   const router = useRouter();
  210 | 
  211 |   const openPlayerToNowPlaying = () => {
  212 |     const slug = (p.queueContextSlug ?? "").trim();
  213 |     const tid = (p.current?.recordingId ?? "").trim();
  214 | 
  215 |     const qs = preservedQueryFromLocation();
  216 | 
  217 |     if (slug && tid) {
  218 |       router.push(
  219 |         `/${encodeURIComponent(slug)}/${encodeURIComponent(tid)}${qs}`,
  220 |         { scroll: false },
  221 |       );
  222 |     } else if (slug) {
  223 |       router.push(`/${encodeURIComponent(slug)}${qs}`, { scroll: false });
  224 |     } else {
  225 |       // Fallback: go to featured album directly (no more /player alias hop)
  226 |       router.push(`${qs}`, { scroll: false });
  227 |     }
  228 | 
  229 |     onExpand?.();
  230 |   };
  231 | 
  232 |   const { shareTarget } = useShareUX();
  233 | 
  234 |   const [mounted, setMounted] = React.useState(false);
  235 |   React.useEffect(() => setMounted(true), []);
  236 | 
  237 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  238 |     if (!mounted) return;
  239 |     if (typeof document === "undefined") return;
  240 | 
  241 |     const el = document.getElementById("af-mini-player");
  242 |     if (!el) return;
  243 | 
  244 |     let raf: number | null = null;
  245 |     let ro: ResizeObserver | null = null;
  246 | 
  247 |     const applyNow = () => {
  248 |       // don’t force layout reads while backgrounded
  249 |       if (document.hidden) return;
  250 |       const h = Math.ceil(el.getBoundingClientRect().height || 0);
  251 |       document.documentElement.style.setProperty(
  252 |         "--af-mini-player-h",
  253 |         `${h}px`,
  254 |       );
  255 |     };
  256 | 
  257 |     const schedule = () => {
  258 |       if (raf != null) return;
  259 |       raf = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
  260 |         raf = null;
  261 |         applyNow();
  262 |       });
  263 |     };
  264 | 
  265 |     // prime once
  266 |     schedule();
  267 | 
  268 |     ro = new ResizeObserver(() => schedule());
  269 |     ro.observe(el);
  270 | 
  271 |     const onResize = () => schedule();
  272 |     window.addEventListener("resize", onResize, { passive: true });
  273 | 
  274 |     const onVis = () => {
  275 |       if (!document.hidden) schedule();
  276 |     };
  277 |     document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVis, { passive: true });
  278 | 
  279 |     return () => {
  280 |       if (raf != null) window.cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
  281 |       raf = null;
  282 |       ro?.disconnect();
  283 |       ro = null;
  284 |       window.removeEventListener("resize", onResize);
  285 |       document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVis);
  286 |     };
  287 |   }, [mounted]);
  288 | 
  289 |   const playingish =
  290 |     p.status === "playing" ||
  291 |     p.status === "loading" ||
  292 |     p.intent === "play" ||
  293 |     p.playRequestPending;
  294 | 
  295 |   // Pending-first display (truthy UI during transitions)
  296 |   const pendingTrack = findTrackById(p.queue, p.pendingRecordingId) ?? null;
  297 |   const displayTrack = pendingTrack ?? p.current ?? null;
  298 | 
  299 |   const resolvedArtworkUrl = artworkUrl ?? p.queueContextArtworkUrl ?? null;
  300 | 
  301 |   /* ---------------- Small anti-doubletap locks ---------------- */
  302 | 
  303 |   const [transportLock, setTransportLock] = React.useState(false);
  304 |   const lockFor = (ms: number) => {
  305 |     setTransportLock(true);
  306 |     window.setTimeout(() => setTransportLock(false), ms);
  307 |   };
  308 | 
  309 |   const [playLock, setPlayLock] = React.useState(false);
  310 |   const lockPlayFor = (ms: number) => {
  311 |     setPlayLock(true);
  312 |     window.setTimeout(() => setPlayLock(false), ms);
  313 |   };
  314 | 
  315 |   /* ---------------- Seek (based on current track, not pending) ---------------- */
  316 | 
  317 |   const curId = p.current?.recordingId ?? "";
  318 |   const durMs = Number(
  319 |     (p.durationByRecordingId?.[curId] ?? p.current?.durationMs ?? 0) || 0,
  320 |   );
  321 |   const durKnown = durMs > 0;
  322 |   const durSec = Math.max(1, Math.round(durMs / 1000));
  323 | 
  324 |   const idx = curId ? p.queue.findIndex((t) => t.recordingId === curId) : -1;
  325 |   const atStart = idx <= 0;
  326 |   const atEnd = idx >= 0 && idx === p.queue.length - 1;
  327 | 
  328 |   const prevDisabled = !p.current || transportLock || atStart;
  329 |   const nextDisabled = !p.current || transportLock || atEnd;
  330 | 
  331 |   const posSecReal = (p.positionMs ?? 0) / 1000;
  332 |   const safePosReal = durKnown ? clamp(posSecReal, 0, durSec) : 0;
  333 | 
  334 |   const pendingSec = p.pendingSeekMs != null ? p.pendingSeekMs / 1000 : null;
  335 |   const safePending =
  336 |     pendingSec != null && durKnown
  337 |       ? clamp(pendingSec, 0, durSec)
  338 |       : (pendingSec ?? undefined);
  339 | 
  340 |   const [scrubbing, setScrubbing] = React.useState(false);
  341 |   const [scrubSec, setScrubSec] = React.useState(0);
  342 | 
  343 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  344 |     setScrubbing(false);
  345 |     setScrubSec(0);
  346 |   }, [p.current?.recordingId]);
  347 | 
  348 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  349 |     if (!scrubbing) setScrubSec(safePosReal);
  350 |   }, [safePosReal, scrubbing]);
  351 | 
  352 |   const sliderValue = scrubbing ? scrubSec : (safePending ?? safePosReal);
  353 | 
  354 |   /* ---------------- Seek tooltip ---------------- */
  355 | 
  356 |   const seekWrapRef = React.useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  357 |   const [seekTip, setSeekTip] = React.useState<{
  358 |     open: boolean;
  359 |     sec: number;
  360 |     x: number;
  361 |     y: number;
  362 |   }>({
  363 |     open: false,
  364 |     sec: 0,
  365 |     x: 0,
  366 |     y: 0,
  367 |   });
  368 | 
  369 |   const computeSeekTipFromClientX = React.useCallback(
  370 |     (clientX: number, forceOpen: boolean) => {
  371 |       if (!durKnown) return;
  372 |       const wrap = seekWrapRef.current;
  373 |       if (!wrap) return;
  374 |       const r = wrap.getBoundingClientRect();
  375 |       const pct = clamp((clientX - r.left) / Math.max(1, r.width), 0, 1);
  376 |       const sec = clamp(Math.round(pct * durSec), 0, durSec);
  377 |       const x = r.left + pct * r.width;
  378 |       const y = r.top;
  379 |       setSeekTip({ open: forceOpen, sec, x, y });
  380 |       if (scrubbing) setScrubSec(sec);
  381 |     },
  382 |     [durKnown, durSec, scrubbing],
  383 |   );
  384 | 
  385 |   const closeSeekTip = React.useCallback(() => {
  386 |     setSeekTip((s) => (s.open ? { ...s, open: false } : s));
  387 |   }, []);
  388 | 
  389 |   /* ---------------- Volume popup ---------------- */
  390 | 
  391 |   const [volOpen, setVolOpen] = React.useState(false);
  392 |   const vol = p.volume;
  393 |   const muted = p.muted || p.volume <= 0.001;
  394 | 
  395 |   const volPopupRef = React.useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  396 | 
  397 |   const volBtnRef = React.useRef<HTMLButtonElement | null>(null);
  398 |   const [volAnchor, setVolAnchor] = React.useState<{
  399 |     x: number;
  400 |     y: number;
  401 |   } | null>(null);
  402 | 
  403 |   React.useLayoutEffect(() => {
  404 |     if (!volOpen) {
  405 |       setVolAnchor(null);
  406 |       return;
  407 |     }
  408 | 
  409 |     const el = volBtnRef.current;
  410 |     if (!el) return;
  411 | 
  412 |     let raf: number | null = null;
  413 | 
  414 |     const computeNow = () => {
  415 |       if (document.hidden) return;
  416 |       const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
  417 |       setVolAnchor({ x: r.left + r.width / 2, y: r.top });
  418 |     };
  419 | 
  420 |     const schedule = () => {
  421 |       if (raf != null) return;
  422 |       raf = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
  423 |         raf = null;
  424 |         computeNow();
  425 |       });
  426 |     };
  427 | 
  428 |     const onPointerDown = (e: PointerEvent) => {
  429 |       const btn = volBtnRef.current;
  430 |       const popup = volPopupRef.current;
  431 |       if (!btn || !popup) return;
  432 | 
  433 |       const t = e.target as Node;
  434 |       if (btn.contains(t)) return;
  435 |       if (popup.contains(t)) return;
  436 | 
  437 |       setVolOpen(false);
  438 |     };
  439 | 
  440 |     // prime
  441 |     schedule();
  442 | 
  443 |     document.addEventListener("pointerdown", onPointerDown, true);
  444 |     window.addEventListener("scroll", schedule, {
  445 |       capture: true,
  446 |       passive: true,
  447 |     });
  448 |     window.addEventListener("resize", schedule, { passive: true });
  449 |     document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", () => {
  450 |       if (!document.hidden) schedule();
  451 |     });
  452 | 
  453 |     return () => {
  454 |       if (raf != null) cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
  455 |       raf = null;
  456 | 
  457 |       document.removeEventListener("pointerdown", onPointerDown, true);
  458 |       window.removeEventListener("scroll", schedule, true);
  459 |       window.removeEventListener("resize", schedule);
  460 |       document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", schedule);
  461 |     };
  462 |   }, [volOpen]);
  463 | 
  464 |   /* ---------------- Copy ---------------- */
  465 | 
  466 |   const title =
  467 |     displayTrack?.title ?? displayTrack?.recordingId ?? "Nothing queued";
  468 | 
  469 |   const statusLine = p.lastError
  470 |     ? "Playback error"
  471 |     : (displayTrack?.artist ?? "");
  472 | 
  473 |   /* ---------------- Share ---------------- */
  474 | 
  475 |   const onShare = async () => {
  476 |     const albumSlug = p.queueContextSlug;
  477 |     if (!albumSlug) return;
  478 |     if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
  479 | 
  480 |     const origin = window.location.origin;
  481 |     const albumTitle =
  482 |       (p.queueContextTitle ?? albumSlug).toString().trim() || albumSlug;
  483 |     const artistName =
  484 |       p.queueContextArtist ??
  485 |       ((displayTrack?.artist ?? "").toString().trim() || undefined);
  486 | 
  487 |     const cur = displayTrack;
  488 |     if (cur?.recordingId) {
  489 |       const target = buildShareTarget({
  490 |         type: "track",
  491 |         methodHint: "sheet",
  492 |         origin,
  493 |         album: {
  494 |           slug: albumSlug,
  495 |           id: p.queueContextId,
  496 |           title: albumTitle,
  497 |           artistName,
  498 |         },
  499 |         track: {
  500 |           recordingId: cur.recordingId,
  501 |           displayId: cur.displayId,
  502 |           title:
  503 |             (cur.title ?? cur.displayId).toString().trim() || cur.displayId,
  504 |         },
  505 |       });
  506 |       await shareTarget(target);
  507 |       return;
  508 |     }
  509 | 
  510 |     const target = buildShareTarget({
  511 |       type: "album",
  512 |       methodHint: "sheet",
  513 |       origin,
  514 |       album: {
  515 |         slug: albumSlug,
  516 |         id: p.queueContextId,
  517 |         title: albumTitle,
  518 |         artistName,
  519 |       },
  520 |     });
  521 |     await shareTarget(target);
  522 |   };

  132 | function clamp(n: number, lo: number, hi: number) {
  133 |   return Math.max(lo, Math.min(hi, n));
  134 | }
  135 | 
  136 | function nextRepeat(r: RepeatMode): RepeatMode {
  137 |   if (r === "off") return "all";
  138 |   if (r === "all") return "one";
  139 |   return "off";

  142 | function positiveDurationMs(value: number | undefined): number {
  143 |   return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0
  144 |     ? Math.round(value)
  145 |     : 0;
  146 | }
  147 | 
  148 | function getAssetDurationMs(
  149 |   assetDurationByPlaybackId: Record<string, number>,
  150 |   track: PlayerTrack | undefined,
  151 | ): number {
  152 |   const playbackId = (track?.muxPlaybackId ?? "").trim();
  153 | 
  154 |   return playbackId
  155 |     ? positiveDurationMs(assetDurationByPlaybackId[playbackId])
  156 |     : 0;
  157 | }
  158 | 
  159 | function getEffectiveDurationMs(
  160 |   state: Pick<
  161 |     PlayerState,
  162 |     "durationByRecordingId" | "assetDurationByPlaybackId"
  163 |   >,
  164 |   track: PlayerTrack | undefined,
  165 | ): number {
  166 |   const assetDurationMs = getAssetDurationMs(
  167 |     state.assetDurationByPlaybackId,
  168 |     track,
  169 |   );
  170 | 
  171 |   if (assetDurationMs > 0) return assetDurationMs;
  172 | 
  173 |   const recordingId = (track?.recordingId ?? "").trim();
  174 |   const catalogueDurationMs = recordingId
  175 |     ? positiveDurationMs(state.durationByRecordingId[recordingId])
  176 |     : 0;
  177 | 
  178 |   return catalogueDurationMs || positiveDurationMs(track?.durationMs);
  179 | }
  180 | 
  181 | function retainAssetDurationsByPlaybackId(
  182 |   previous: Record<string, number>,
  183 |   tracks: PlayerTrack[],
  184 | ): Record<string, number> {
  185 |   const activePlaybackIds = new Set<string>();
  186 | 
  187 |   for (const track of tracks) {
  188 |     const playbackId = (track.muxPlaybackId ?? "").trim();
  189 |     if (playbackId) activePlaybackIds.add(playbackId);
  190 |   }
  191 | 
  192 |   let next = previous;
  193 | 
  194 |   for (const playbackId of Object.keys(previous)) {
  195 |     if (activePlaybackIds.has(playbackId)) continue;
  196 | 
  197 |     if (next === previous) next = { ...previous };
  198 |     delete next[playbackId];
  199 |   }
  200 | 
  201 |   return next;
  202 | }
  203 | 
  204 | function hydrateTrack(
  205 |   t: PlayerTrack,
  206 |   durationByRecordingId: Record<string, number>,
  207 | ): PlayerTrack {
  208 |   const cached = durationByRecordingId[t.recordingId];
  209 |   if (!cached || cached <= 0) return t;
  210 |   if (t.durationMs === cached) return t;
  211 |   return { ...t, durationMs: cached };
  212 | }
  213 | 
  214 | function hydrateTracks(
  215 |   ts: PlayerTrack[],
  216 |   durationByRecordingId: Record<string, number>,
  217 | ) {
  218 |   let changed = false;
  219 |   const next = ts.map((t) => {
  220 |     const ht = hydrateTrack(t, durationByRecordingId);
  221 |     if (ht !== t) changed = true;
  222 |     return ht;
  223 |   });
  224 |   return changed ? next : ts;
  225 | }

  227 | function blockedPlaybackMessage(access: PlaybackAccessDecision): string {
  228 |   if (access.embargoed) {
  229 |     return "Playback is disabled pre-release.";
  230 |   }
  231 | 
  232 |   if (access.reason) return access.reason;
  233 | 
  234 |   return "Playback is not available for this release.";
  235 | }

  237 | type TargetedPlayIntentDetail = {

  241 | 
  242 | function dispatchTargetedPlayIntent(
  243 |   track: PlayerTrack,
  244 |   state: Pick<PlayerState, "queueContextId">,
  245 | ): void {
  246 |   if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
  247 | 
  248 |   const albumId = (state.queueContextId ?? "").trim() || null;
  249 | 
  250 |   window.dispatchEvent(
  251 |     new CustomEvent<TargetedPlayIntentDetail>("af:play-target-intent", {
  252 |       detail: {
  253 |         track,
  254 |         albumId,
  255 |       },
  256 |     }),
  257 |   );
  258 | }
  259 | 
  260 | function getNextTransportTrack(state: PlayerState): PlayerTrack | undefined {
  261 |   const current = state.current;
  262 | 
  263 |   if (!current || state.queue.length === 0) return undefined;
  264 |   if (state.repeat === "one") return current;
  265 | 
  266 |   const index = state.queue.findIndex(
  267 |     (track) => track.recordingId === current.recordingId,
  268 |   );
  269 |   const currentIndex = index >= 0 ? index : 0;
  270 | 
  271 |   if (currentIndex + 1 < state.queue.length) {
  272 |     return state.queue[currentIndex + 1];
  273 |   }
  274 | 
  275 |   return state.repeat === "all" ? state.queue[0] : undefined;
  276 | }
  277 | 
  278 | function getPreviousTransportTrack(
  279 |   state: PlayerState,
  280 | ): PlayerTrack | undefined {
  281 |   const current = state.current;
  282 | 
  283 |   if (!current || state.queue.length === 0) return undefined;
  284 | 
  285 |   if (state.status === "playing" && state.positionMs > 3000) {
  286 |     return current;
  287 |   }
  288 | 
  289 |   const index = state.queue.findIndex(
  290 |     (track) => track.recordingId === current.recordingId,
  291 |   );
  292 |   const currentIndex = index >= 0 ? index : 0;
  293 | 
  294 |   if (currentIndex > 0) {
  295 |     return state.queue[currentIndex - 1];
  296 |   }
  297 | 
  298 |   if (state.repeat === "all") {
  299 |     return state.queue[state.queue.length - 1];
  300 |   }
  301 | 
  302 |   return current;
  303 | }
  304 | 
  305 | function primeDurationByRecordingId(
  306 |   prev: Record<string, number>,
  307 |   tracks: PlayerTrack[],
  308 | ): Record<string, number> {
  309 |   let next = prev;
  310 |   for (const t of tracks) {
  311 |     if (!t?.recordingId) continue;
  312 |     const ms = t.durationMs;
  313 |     if (typeof ms !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(ms) || ms <= 0) continue;
  314 |     if (typeof next[t.recordingId] === "number" && next[t.recordingId] > 0)
  315 |       continue;
  316 |     if (next === prev) next = { ...prev };
  317 |     next[t.recordingId] = ms;
  318 |   }
  319 |   return next;
  320 | }

  322 | export function PlayerStateProvider(props: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  323 |   const {
  324 |     isLoaded: clerkAuthLoaded,
  325 |     isSignedIn,
  326 |     userId,
  327 |     sessionId,
  328 |   } = useAuth();
  329 | 
  330 |   const playbackAccessIdentityKey = !clerkAuthLoaded
  331 |     ? "clerk:loading"
  332 |     : isSignedIn
  333 |       ? `clerk:user:${userId ?? ""}:session:${sessionId ?? ""}`
  334 |       : "clerk:anonymous";
  335 | 
  336 |   const [state, setState] = React.useState<PlayerState>({
  337 |     status: "idle",
  338 |     current: undefined,
  339 |     queue: [],
  340 |     lastError: undefined,
  341 |     lastPlayAttemptAtMs: undefined,
  342 |     playRequestPending: false,
  343 | 
  344 |     queueContextId: undefined,
  345 |     queueContextSlug: undefined,
  346 |     queueContextTitle: undefined,
  347 |     queueContextArtist: undefined,
  348 |     queueContextArtworkUrl: null,
  349 |     queueSharePlaybackContext: null,
  350 |     queueSharePlaybackScopeId: null,
  351 | 
  352 |     intent: null,
  353 |     intentAtMs: undefined,
  354 |     selectedRecordingId: undefined,
  355 |     pendingRecordingId: undefined,
  356 | 
  357 |     pendingSeekMs: undefined,
  358 |     seeking: false,
  359 | 
  360 |     loadingReason: undefined,
  361 |     reloadNonce: 0,
  362 | 
  363 |     positionMs: 0,
  364 |     seekNonce: 0,
  365 | 
  366 |     volume: 0.9,
  367 |     muted: false,
  368 |     repeat: "off",
  369 | 
  370 |     durationByRecordingId: {},
  371 |     assetDurationByPlaybackId: {},
  372 |   });
  373 | 
  374 |   const stateRef = React.useRef(state);
  375 |   const playRequestSequenceRef = React.useRef(0);
  376 | 
  377 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  378 |     stateRef.current = state;
  379 |   }, [state]);
  380 | 
  381 |   const blockPlayback = React.useCallback((message: string) => {
  382 |     setState((s) => ({
  383 |       ...s,
  384 |       status: s.status === "loading" ? "idle" : s.status,
  385 |       intent: "pause",
  386 |       intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  387 |       playRequestPending: false,
  388 |       loadingReason: undefined,
  389 |       pendingRecordingId: undefined,
  390 |       lastError: message,
  391 |     }));
  392 |   }, []);
  393 | 
  394 |   const guardPlayback = React.useCallback(
  395 |     async (onAllowed: () => void): Promise<void> => {
  396 |       const catalogueId = (stateRef.current.queueContextId ?? "").trim();
  397 | 
  398 |       if (!catalogueId) {
  399 |         onAllowed();
  400 |         return;
  401 |       }
  402 | 
  403 |       try {
  404 |         const access = await fetchPlaybackAccessDecision({
  405 |           catalogueId,
  406 |           shareToken: readPlaybackShareTokenFromLocation(),
  407 |           accessIdentityKey: playbackAccessIdentityKey,
  408 |         });
  409 | 
  410 |         if (!access.allowed) {
  411 |           blockPlayback(blockedPlaybackMessage(access));
  412 |           return;
  413 |         }
  414 | 
  415 |         const expectedSharePlaybackScopeId = `alb:${catalogueId.replace(
  416 |           /^alb:/i,
  417 |           "",
  418 |         )}`;
  419 | 
  420 |         if (
  421 |           access.sharePlaybackContext &&
  422 |           access.sharePlaybackScopeId === expectedSharePlaybackScopeId
  423 |         ) {
  424 |           setState((current) => ({
  425 |             ...current,
  426 |             queueSharePlaybackContext: access.sharePlaybackContext,
  427 |             queueSharePlaybackScopeId: access.sharePlaybackScopeId,
  428 |           }));
  429 |         }
  430 | 
  431 |         onAllowed();
  432 |       } catch {
  433 |         blockPlayback("Playback access could not be verified.");
  434 |       }
  435 |     },
  436 |     [blockPlayback, playbackAccessIdentityKey],
  437 |   );
  438 |   const actions: PlayerActions = React.useMemo(() => {
  439 |     return {
  440 |       setIntent: (i: Intent) =>
  441 |         setState((s) => ({
  442 |           ...s,
  443 |           intent: i,
  444 |           intentAtMs: i ? Date.now() : undefined,
  445 |         })),
  446 | 
  447 |       clearIntent: () =>
  448 |         setState((s) =>
  449 |           s.intent ? { ...s, intent: null, intentAtMs: undefined } : s,
  450 |         ),
  451 | 
  452 |       selectTrack: (id?: string) =>
  453 |         setState((s) => {
  454 |           if (s.selectedRecordingId === id) return s;
  455 |           return {
  456 |             ...s,
  457 |             selectedRecordingId: id,
  458 |           };
  459 |         }),
  460 | 
  461 |       setPendingRecordingId: (id?: string) =>
  462 |         setState((s) => {
  463 |           if (s.pendingRecordingId === id) return s;
  464 |           return {
  465 |             ...s,
  466 |             pendingRecordingId: id,
  467 |           };
  468 |         }),
  469 | 
  470 |       resolvePendingTrack: (id: string) =>
  471 |         setState((s) => {
  472 |           if (s.pendingRecordingId !== id) return s;
  473 |           return { ...s, pendingRecordingId: undefined };
  474 |         }),
  475 | 
  476 |       play: (track?: PlayerTrack) => {
  477 |         const requestSequence = ++playRequestSequenceRef.current;
  478 |         const requestStartedAtMs = Date.now();
  479 |         const snapshot = stateRef.current;
  480 |         const requestedTrack = track ?? snapshot.current ?? snapshot.queue[0];
  481 | 
  482 |         if (requestedTrack) {
  483 |           dispatchTargetedPlayIntent(
  484 |             hydrateTrack(requestedTrack, snapshot.durationByRecordingId),
  485 |             snapshot,
  486 |           );
  487 |         }
  488 | 
  489 |         setState((s) => ({
  490 |           ...s,
  491 |           playRequestPending: true,
  492 |           lastPlayAttemptAtMs: requestStartedAtMs,
  493 |           lastError: undefined,
  494 |         }));
  495 | 
  496 |         void guardPlayback(() => {
  497 |           if (requestSequence !== playRequestSequenceRef.current) {
  498 |             return;
  499 |           }
  500 | 
  501 |           setState((s) => {
  502 |             if (requestSequence !== playRequestSequenceRef.current) {
  503 |               return s;
  504 |             }
  505 | 
  506 |             const now = Date.now();
  507 |             const rawNext = track ?? s.current ?? s.queue[0];
  508 | 
  509 |             if (!rawNext) {
  510 |               return {
  511 |                 ...s,
  512 |                 status: "idle",
  513 |                 current: undefined,
  514 |                 positionMs: 0,
  515 |                 intent: "play",
  516 |                 intentAtMs: now,
  517 |                 lastPlayAttemptAtMs: now,
  518 |                 playRequestPending: false,
  519 |                 lastError: undefined,
  520 |                 loadingReason: undefined,
  521 |                 pendingRecordingId: undefined,
  522 |               };
  523 |             }
  524 | 
  525 |             const nextTrack = hydrateTrack(rawNext, s.durationByRecordingId);
  526 |             const sameTrack = Boolean(
  527 |               s.current && s.current.recordingId === nextTrack.recordingId,
  528 |             );
  529 | 
  530 |             const base = {
  531 |               ...s,
  532 |               intent: "play" as const,
  533 |               intentAtMs: now,
  534 |               lastPlayAttemptAtMs: now,
  535 |               playRequestPending: false,
  536 |               lastError: undefined,
  537 |               selectedRecordingId: nextTrack.recordingId,
  538 |               pendingRecordingId: nextTrack.recordingId,
  539 |             };
  540 | 
  541 |             if (sameTrack && s.status === "paused") {
  542 |               return {
  543 |                 ...base,
  544 |                 current: hydrateTrack(s.current!, s.durationByRecordingId),
  545 |                 status: "paused",
  546 |                 loadingReason: undefined,
  547 |               };
  548 |             }
  549 | 
  550 |             if (
  551 |               sameTrack &&
  552 |               (s.status === "playing" || s.status === "loading")
  553 |             ) {
  554 |               return { ...base, loadingReason: s.loadingReason };
  555 |             }
  556 | 
  557 |             return {
  558 |               ...base,
  559 |               current: nextTrack,
  560 |               status: "loading",
  561 |               loadingReason: "token",
  562 |               positionMs: 0,
  563 |             };
  564 |           });
  565 |         });
  566 |       },
  567 | 
  568 |       pause: () => {
  569 |         playRequestSequenceRef.current += 1;
  570 | 
  571 |         setState((s) => ({
  572 |           ...s,
  573 |           intent: "pause",
  574 |           intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  575 |           playRequestPending: false,
  576 |         }));
  577 |       },
  578 | 
  579 |       next: () => {
  580 |         const snapshot = stateRef.current;
  581 |         const targetTrack = getNextTransportTrack(snapshot);
  582 | 
  583 |         if (targetTrack) {
  584 |           dispatchTargetedPlayIntent(
  585 |             hydrateTrack(targetTrack, snapshot.durationByRecordingId),
  586 |             snapshot,
  587 |           );
  588 |         }
  589 | 
  590 |         void guardPlayback(() => {
  591 |           setState((s) => {
  592 |             const cur = s.current;
  593 |             if (!cur || s.queue.length === 0) return s;
  594 | 
  595 |             const idx = s.queue.findIndex(
  596 |               (t) => t.recordingId === cur.recordingId,
  597 |             );
  598 |             const at = idx >= 0 ? idx : 0;
  599 | 
  600 |             if (s.repeat === "one") {
  601 |               return {
  602 |                 ...s,
  603 |                 status: "loading",
  604 |                 loadingReason: "attach",
  605 |                 positionMs: 0,
  606 |                 intent: "play",
  607 |                 intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  608 |                 pendingRecordingId: cur.recordingId,
  609 |                 selectedRecordingId: cur.recordingId,
  610 |               };
  611 |             }
  612 | 
  613 |             const nextIdx = at + 1;
  614 |             if (nextIdx < s.queue.length) {
  615 |               const t = hydrateTrack(s.queue[nextIdx], s.durationByRecordingId);
  616 |               return {
  617 |                 ...s,
  618 |                 current: t,
  619 |                 status: "loading",
  620 |                 loadingReason: "token",
  621 |                 positionMs: 0,
  622 |                 intent: "play",
  623 |                 intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  624 |                 pendingRecordingId: t.recordingId,
  625 |                 selectedRecordingId: t.recordingId,
  626 |               };
  627 |             }
  628 | 
  629 |             if (s.repeat === "all" && s.queue.length > 0) {
  630 |               const t = hydrateTrack(s.queue[0], s.durationByRecordingId);
  631 |               return {
  632 |                 ...s,
  633 |                 current: t,
  634 |                 status: "loading",
  635 |                 loadingReason: "token",
  636 |                 positionMs: 0,
  637 |                 intent: "play",
  638 |                 intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  639 |                 pendingRecordingId: t.recordingId,
  640 |                 selectedRecordingId: t.recordingId,
  641 |               };
  642 |             }
  643 | 
  644 |             return {
  645 |               ...s,
  646 |               status: "paused",
  647 |               intent: "pause",
  648 |               intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  649 |               positionMs: 0,
  650 |               pendingSeekMs: 0,
  651 |               seeking: true,
  652 |               seekNonce: s.seekNonce + 1,
  653 |               loadingReason: undefined,
  654 |               pendingRecordingId: undefined,
  655 |             };
  656 |           });
  657 |         });
  658 |       },
  659 | 
  660 |       advanceFromEngine: () => {
  661 |         setState((s) => {
  662 |           const cur = s.current;
  663 |           if (!cur || s.queue.length === 0) return s;
  664 | 
  665 |           const idx = s.queue.findIndex(
  666 |             (t) => t.recordingId === cur.recordingId,
  667 |           );
  668 |           const at = idx >= 0 ? idx : 0;
  669 | 
  670 |           if (s.repeat === "one") {
  671 |             return {
  672 |               ...s,
  673 |               status: "loading",
  674 |               loadingReason: "attach",
  675 |               positionMs: 0,
  676 |               intent: "play",
  677 |               intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  678 |               pendingRecordingId: cur.recordingId,
  679 |               selectedRecordingId: cur.recordingId,
  680 |             };
  681 |           }
  682 | 
  683 |           const nextIdx = at + 1;
  684 | 
  685 |           if (nextIdx < s.queue.length) {
  686 |             const t = hydrateTrack(s.queue[nextIdx], s.durationByRecordingId);
  687 |             return {
  688 |               ...s,
  689 |               current: t,
  690 |               status: "loading",
  691 |               loadingReason: "attach",
  692 |               positionMs: 0,
  693 |               intent: "play",
  694 |               intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  695 |               pendingRecordingId: t.recordingId,
  696 |               selectedRecordingId: t.recordingId,
  697 |             };
  698 |           }
  699 | 
  700 |           if (s.repeat === "all" && s.queue.length > 0) {
  701 |             const t = hydrateTrack(s.queue[0], s.durationByRecordingId);
  702 |             return {
  703 |               ...s,
  704 |               current: t,
  705 |               status: "loading",
  706 |               loadingReason: "attach",
  707 |               positionMs: 0,
  708 |               intent: "play",
  709 |               intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  710 |               pendingRecordingId: t.recordingId,
  711 |               selectedRecordingId: t.recordingId,
  712 |             };
  713 |           }
  714 | 
  715 |           return {
  716 |             ...s,
  717 |             status: "paused",
  718 |             intent: null,
  719 |             intentAtMs: undefined,
  720 |             positionMs: 0,
  721 |             pendingSeekMs: 0,
  722 |             seeking: true,
  723 |             seekNonce: s.seekNonce + 1,
  724 |             loadingReason: undefined,
  725 |             pendingRecordingId: undefined,
  726 |           };
  727 |         });
  728 |       },
  729 | 
  730 |       prev: () => {
  731 |         const snapshot = stateRef.current;
  732 |         const targetTrack = getPreviousTransportTrack(snapshot);
  733 | 
  734 |         if (targetTrack) {
  735 |           dispatchTargetedPlayIntent(
  736 |             hydrateTrack(targetTrack, snapshot.durationByRecordingId),
  737 |             snapshot,
  738 |           );
  739 |         }
  740 | 
  741 |         void guardPlayback(() => {
  742 |           setState((s) => {
  743 |             const cur = s.current;
  744 |             if (!cur || s.queue.length === 0) return s;
  745 | 
  746 |             if (s.status === "playing" && s.positionMs > 3000) {
  747 |               return {
  748 |                 ...s,
  749 |                 positionMs: 0,
  750 |                 status: "loading",
  751 |                 loadingReason: "attach",
  752 |                 intent: "play",
  753 |                 intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  754 |                 pendingRecordingId: cur.recordingId,
  755 |                 selectedRecordingId: cur.recordingId,
  756 |               };
  757 |             }
  758 | 
  759 |             const idx = s.queue.findIndex(
  760 |               (t) => t.recordingId === cur.recordingId,
  761 |             );
  762 |             const at = idx >= 0 ? idx : 0;
  763 |             const prevIdx = at - 1;
  764 | 
  765 |             if (prevIdx >= 0) {
  766 |               const t = hydrateTrack(s.queue[prevIdx], s.durationByRecordingId);
  767 |               return {
  768 |                 ...s,
  769 |                 current: t,
  770 |                 status: "loading",
  771 |                 loadingReason: "token",
  772 |                 positionMs: 0,
  773 |                 intent: "play",
  774 |                 intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  775 |                 pendingRecordingId: t.recordingId,
  776 |                 selectedRecordingId: t.recordingId,
  777 |               };
  778 |             }
  779 | 
  780 |             if (s.repeat === "all" && s.queue.length > 0) {
  781 |               const t = hydrateTrack(
  782 |                 s.queue[s.queue.length - 1],
  783 |                 s.durationByRecordingId,
  784 |               );
  785 |               return {
  786 |                 ...s,
  787 |                 current: t,
  788 |                 status: "loading",
  789 |                 loadingReason: "token",
  790 |                 positionMs: 0,
  791 |                 intent: "play",
  792 |                 intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  793 |                 pendingRecordingId: t.recordingId,
  794 |                 selectedRecordingId: t.recordingId,
  795 |               };
  796 |             }
  797 | 
  798 |             return {
  799 |               ...s,
  800 |               positionMs: 0,
  801 |               status: "loading",
  802 |               loadingReason: "attach",
  803 |               intent: "play",
  804 |               intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  805 |               pendingRecordingId: cur.recordingId,
  806 |               selectedRecordingId: cur.recordingId,
  807 |             };
  808 |           });
  809 |         });
  810 |       },
  811 | 
  812 |       setQueue: (tracks: PlayerTrack[], opts?: QueueContext) =>
  813 |         setState((s) => {
  814 |           const nextDurationByRecordingId = primeDurationByRecordingId(
  815 |             s.durationByRecordingId,
  816 |             tracks,
  817 |           );
  818 |           const nextAssetDurationByPlaybackId =
  819 |             retainAssetDurationsByPlaybackId(
  820 |               s.assetDurationByPlaybackId,
  821 |               tracks,
  822 |             );
  823 |           const hydratedQueue = hydrateTracks(
  824 |             tracks,
  825 |             nextDurationByRecordingId,
  826 |           );
  827 | 
  828 |           const initialCurrentRecordingId =
  829 |             typeof opts?.initialCurrentRecordingId === "string"
  830 |               ? opts.initialCurrentRecordingId.trim()
  831 |               : "";
  832 | 
  833 |           const requestedInitialCurrent = initialCurrentRecordingId
  834 |             ? (hydratedQueue.find(
  835 |                 (track) => track.recordingId === initialCurrentRecordingId,
  836 |               ) ?? null)
  837 |             : null;
  838 | 
  839 |           const nextCurrentRaw =
  840 |             requestedInitialCurrent ?? s.current ?? hydratedQueue[0];
  841 | 
  842 |           const nextCurrent = nextCurrentRaw
  843 |             ? hydrateTrack(nextCurrentRaw, nextDurationByRecordingId)
  844 |             : undefined;
  845 | 
  846 |           const slug =
  847 |             typeof opts?.contextSlug === "string"
  848 |               ? opts.contextSlug.trim()
  849 |               : "";
  850 |           const title =
  851 |             typeof opts?.contextTitle === "string"
  852 |               ? opts.contextTitle.trim()
  853 |               : "";
  854 |           const artist =
  855 |             typeof opts?.contextArtist === "string"
  856 |               ? opts.contextArtist.trim()
  857 |               : "";
  858 | 
  859 |           const hasSlug = slug.length > 0;
  860 |           const hasTitle = title.length > 0;
  861 |           const hasArtist = artist.length > 0;
  862 |           const hasArtwork = typeof opts?.artworkUrl !== "undefined";
  863 |           const hasSharePlaybackContext = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(
  864 |             opts ?? {},
  865 |             "sharePlaybackContext",
  866 |           );
  867 | 
  868 |           const hasSharePlaybackScopeId = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(
  869 |             opts ?? {},
  870 |             "sharePlaybackScopeId",
  871 |           );
  872 |           const hasId =
  873 |             typeof opts?.contextId === "string" && opts.contextId.length > 0;
  874 | 
  875 |           return {
  876 |             ...s,
  877 |             durationByRecordingId: nextDurationByRecordingId,
  878 |             assetDurationByPlaybackId: nextAssetDurationByPlaybackId,
  879 |             queue: hydratedQueue,
  880 | 
  881 |             queueContextId: hasId ? opts!.contextId : s.queueContextId,
  882 |             queueContextSlug: hasSlug ? slug : s.queueContextSlug,
  883 |             queueContextTitle: hasTitle ? title : s.queueContextTitle,
  884 |             queueContextArtist: hasArtist ? artist : s.queueContextArtist,
  885 |             queueContextArtworkUrl: hasArtwork
  886 |               ? (opts!.artworkUrl ?? null)
  887 |               : (s.queueContextArtworkUrl ?? null),
  888 |             queueSharePlaybackContext: hasSharePlaybackContext
  889 |               ? typeof opts?.sharePlaybackContext === "string" &&
  890 |                 opts.sharePlaybackContext.trim()
  891 |                 ? opts.sharePlaybackContext.trim()
  892 |                 : null
  893 |               : s.queueSharePlaybackContext,
  894 | 
  895 |             queueSharePlaybackScopeId: hasSharePlaybackScopeId
  896 |               ? typeof opts?.sharePlaybackScopeId === "string" &&
  897 |                 opts.sharePlaybackScopeId.trim()
  898 |                 ? opts.sharePlaybackScopeId.trim()
  899 |                 : null
  900 |               : s.queueSharePlaybackScopeId,
  901 | 
  902 |             current: nextCurrent,
  903 |             positionMs: requestedInitialCurrent
  904 |               ? 0
  905 |               : s.current
  906 |                 ? s.positionMs
  907 |                 : 0,
  908 |             selectedRecordingId: requestedInitialCurrent
  909 |               ? requestedInitialCurrent.recordingId
  910 |               : (s.selectedRecordingId ?? nextCurrent?.recordingId),
  911 |             pendingRecordingId: requestedInitialCurrent
  912 |               ? undefined
  913 |               : s.pendingRecordingId,
  914 |           };
  915 |         }),
  916 | 
  917 |       enqueue: (track: PlayerTrack) =>
  918 |         setState((s) => {
  919 |           const nextDurationByRecordingId = primeDurationByRecordingId(
  920 |             s.durationByRecordingId,
  921 |             [track],
  922 |           );
  923 |           const t = hydrateTrack(track, nextDurationByRecordingId);
  924 |           return {
  925 |             ...s,
  926 |             durationByRecordingId: nextDurationByRecordingId,
  927 |             queue: [...s.queue, t],
  928 |             current: s.current ?? t,
  929 |             selectedRecordingId: s.selectedRecordingId ?? t.recordingId,
  930 |           };
  931 |         }),
  932 | 
  933 |       clearQueue: () =>
  934 |         setState((s) => ({
  935 |           ...s,
  936 |           queue: [],
  937 |           current: undefined,
  938 |           selectedRecordingId: undefined,
  939 |           pendingRecordingId: undefined,
  940 | 
  941 |           positionMs: 0,
  942 |           pendingSeekMs: undefined,
  943 |           seeking: false,
  944 |           seekNonce: s.seekNonce + 1,
  945 |           loadingReason: undefined,
  946 |           queueSharePlaybackContext: null,
  947 |           queueSharePlaybackScopeId: null,
  948 |           assetDurationByPlaybackId: {},
  949 |         })),
  950 | 
  951 |       setPositionMs: (ms: number) =>
  952 |         setState((s) => ({
  953 |           ...s,
  954 |           positionMs: Math.max(0, ms),
  955 |         })),
  956 | 
  957 |       setDurationMs: (ms: number) =>
  958 |         setState((s) => {
  959 |           const cur = s.current;
  960 |           if (!cur) return s;
  961 |           if (!Number.isFinite(ms) || ms <= 0) return s;
  962 | 
  963 |           const alreadyCached =
  964 |             typeof s.durationByRecordingId[cur.recordingId] === "number" &&
  965 |             s.durationByRecordingId[cur.recordingId] > 0;
  966 |           const alreadyOnTrack =
  967 |             typeof cur.durationMs === "number" && cur.durationMs > 0;
  968 |           if (alreadyCached || alreadyOnTrack) return s;
  969 | 
  970 |           const nextDurationByRecordingId = {
  971 |             ...s.durationByRecordingId,
  972 |             [cur.recordingId]: ms,
  973 |           };
  974 | 
  975 |           const nextCurrent = { ...cur, durationMs: ms };
  976 |           let changed = false;
  977 |           const nextQueue = s.queue.map((t) => {
  978 |             if (t.recordingId !== cur.recordingId) return t;
  979 |             if (typeof t.durationMs === "number" && t.durationMs > 0) return t;
  980 |             changed = true;
  981 |             return { ...t, durationMs: ms };
  982 |           });
  983 | 
  984 |           return {
  985 |             ...s,
  986 |             durationByRecordingId: nextDurationByRecordingId,
  987 |             current: nextCurrent,
  988 |             queue: changed ? nextQueue : s.queue,
  989 |           };
  990 |         }),
  991 | 
  992 |       setAssetDurationMs: (playbackId: string, ms: number) => {
  993 |         const normalizedPlaybackId = playbackId.trim();
  994 |         const normalizedDurationMs = positiveDurationMs(ms);
  995 | 
  996 |         if (!normalizedPlaybackId || normalizedDurationMs <= 0) return;
  997 | 
  998 |         setState((s) => {
  999 |           if (
 1000 |             s.assetDurationByPlaybackId[normalizedPlaybackId] ===
 1001 |             normalizedDurationMs
 1002 |           ) {
 1003 |             return s;
 1004 |           }
 1005 | 
 1006 |           return {
 1007 |             ...s,
 1008 |             assetDurationByPlaybackId: {
 1009 |               ...s.assetDurationByPlaybackId,
 1010 |               [normalizedPlaybackId]: normalizedDurationMs,
 1011 |             },
 1012 |           };
 1013 |         });
 1014 |       },
 1015 | 
 1016 |       setStatusExternal: (st: PlayerStatus) =>
 1017 |         setState((s) => (s.status === st ? s : { ...s, status: st })),
 1018 | 
 1019 |       setLoadingReasonExternal: (r?: LoadingReason) =>
 1020 |         setState((s) =>
 1021 |           s.loadingReason === r ? s : { ...s, loadingReason: r },
 1022 |         ),
 1023 | 
 1024 |       seek: (ms: number) => {
 1025 |         setState((s) => {
 1026 |           const dur = getEffectiveDurationMs(s, s.current);
 1027 |           const next = dur > 0 ? clamp(ms, 0, dur) : Math.max(0, ms);
 1028 |           return {
 1029 |             ...s,
 1030 |             positionMs: next,
 1031 |             pendingSeekMs: next,
 1032 |             seeking: true,
 1033 |             seekNonce: s.seekNonce + 1,
 1034 |           };
 1035 |         });
 1036 |       },
 1037 | 
 1038 |       clearPendingSeek: () =>
 1039 |         setState((s) => {
 1040 |           if (!s.seeking && s.pendingSeekMs == null) return s;
 1041 |           return { ...s, seeking: false, pendingSeekMs: undefined };
 1042 |         }),
 1043 | 
 1044 |       setVolume: (v: number) =>
 1045 |         setState((s) => ({ ...s, volume: clamp(v, 0, 1) })),
 1046 |       toggleMute: () => setState((s) => ({ ...s, muted: !s.muted })),
 1047 | 
 1048 |       cycleRepeat: () =>
 1049 |         setState((s) => ({ ...s, repeat: nextRepeat(s.repeat) })),
 1050 | 
 1051 |       tick: (deltaMs: number) => {
 1052 |         setState((s) => {
 1053 |           if (s.status !== "playing") return s;
 1054 | 
 1055 |           const dur = getEffectiveDurationMs(s, s.current);
 1056 |           const nextPos = Math.max(0, s.positionMs + Math.max(0, deltaMs));
 1057 | 
 1058 |           if (dur <= 0) return { ...s, positionMs: nextPos };
 1059 |           if (nextPos < dur) return { ...s, positionMs: nextPos };
 1060 | 
 1061 |           if (s.repeat === "one") return { ...s, positionMs: 0 };
 1062 | 
 1063 |           const cur = s.current;
 1064 |           const idx = cur
 1065 |             ? s.queue.findIndex((t) => t.recordingId === cur.recordingId)
 1066 |             : -1;
 1067 |           const at = idx >= 0 ? idx : 0;
 1068 |           const nextIdx = at + 1;
 1069 | 
 1070 |           if (nextIdx < s.queue.length) {
 1071 |             const t = hydrateTrack(s.queue[nextIdx], s.durationByRecordingId);
 1072 |             return {
 1073 |               ...s,
 1074 |               current: t,
 1075 |               positionMs: 0,
 1076 |               selectedRecordingId: t.recordingId,
 1077 |               pendingRecordingId: t.recordingId,
 1078 |             };
 1079 |           }
 1080 | 
 1081 |           if (s.repeat === "all" && s.queue.length > 0) {
 1082 |             const t = hydrateTrack(s.queue[0], s.durationByRecordingId);
 1083 |             return {
 1084 |               ...s,
 1085 |               current: t,
 1086 |               positionMs: 0,
 1087 |               selectedRecordingId: t.recordingId,
 1088 |               pendingRecordingId: t.recordingId,
 1089 |             };
 1090 |           }
 1091 | 
 1092 |           return { ...s, status: "paused", positionMs: dur };
 1093 |         });
 1094 |       },
 1095 | 
 1096 |       clearError: () =>
 1097 |         setState((s) => (s.lastError ? { ...s, lastError: undefined } : s)),
 1098 | 
 1099 |       bumpReload: () => {
 1100 |         void guardPlayback(() => {
 1101 |           setState((s) => {
 1102 |             if (!s.current?.muxPlaybackId) {
 1103 |               return { ...s, reloadNonce: s.reloadNonce + 1 };
 1104 |             }
 1105 | 
 1106 |             return {
 1107 |               ...s,
 1108 |               reloadNonce: s.reloadNonce + 1,
 1109 |               status: "loading",
 1110 |               loadingReason: "token",
 1111 |               lastError: undefined,
 1112 |               intent: "play",
 1113 |               intentAtMs: Date.now(),
 1114 |               pendingRecordingId: s.current.recordingId,
 1115 |               selectedRecordingId: s.current.recordingId,
 1116 |             };
 1117 |           });
 1118 |         });
 1119 |       },
 1120 |     };
 1121 |   }, [guardPlayback]);
 1122 | 
 1123 |   const api: PlayerState & PlayerActions = React.useMemo(() => {
 1124 |     return {
 1125 |       ...state,
 1126 |       ...actions,
 1127 |     };
 1128 |   }, [state, actions]);
 1129 | 
 1130 |   const {
 1131 |     status: visualStatus,
 1132 |     intent: visualIntent,
 1133 |     playRequestPending: visualPlayRequestPending,
 1134 |     current: visualCurrent,
 1135 |     queue: visualQueue,
 1136 |   } = state;
 1137 | 
 1138 |   const firstQueuedTrack = visualQueue[0];
 1139 | 
 1140 |   const visualState: PlayerVisualState = React.useMemo(
 1141 |     () => ({
 1142 |       status: visualStatus,
 1143 |       intent: visualIntent,
 1144 |       playRequestPending: visualPlayRequestPending,
 1145 |       current: visualCurrent,
 1146 |       firstQueuedTrack,
 1147 |     }),
 1148 |     [
 1149 |       visualStatus,
 1150 |       visualIntent,
 1151 |       visualPlayRequestPending,
 1152 |       visualCurrent,
 1153 |       firstQueuedTrack,
 1154 |     ],
 1155 |   );
 1156 | 
 1157 |   const currentId = state.current?.recordingId ?? null;
 1158 |   const pendingId = state.pendingRecordingId ?? null;
 1159 | 
 1160 |   React.useEffect(() => {
 1161 |     const id = (currentId ?? "").trim();
 1162 |     if (!id) return;
 1163 |     void ensureLyricsForTrack(id);
 1164 |   }, [currentId]);
 1165 | 
 1166 |   React.useEffect(() => {
 1167 |     const id = (pendingId ?? "").trim();
 1168 |     if (!id) return;
 1169 |     void ensureLyricsForTrack(id);
 1170 |   }, [pendingId]);
 1171 | 
 1172 |   return (
 1173 |     <PlayerActionsCtx.Provider value={actions}>
 1174 |       <PlayerVisualCtx.Provider value={visualState}>
 1175 |         <PlayerCtx.Provider value={api}>{props.children}</PlayerCtx.Provider>
 1176 |       </PlayerVisualCtx.Provider>
 1177 |     </PlayerActionsCtx.Provider>
 1178 |   );
 1179 | }

 1184 |     throw new Error("usePlayer must be used within PlayerStateProvider");
 1185 |   return ctx;

    4 | import React from "react";
    5 | import { usePlayerActions, usePlayerVisual } from "./PlayerState";

   10 | import { mediaSurface } from "./mediaSurface";
   11 | import { useLyricsSnapshot } from "./lyrics/useLyricsSurface";
   12 | 
   13 | type CuesByRecordingId = Record<string, LyricCue[]>;
   14 | type OffsetByRecordingId = Record<string, number>;
   15 | 
   16 | function pickKeyWithCues(
   17 |   cuesByRecordingId: CuesByRecordingId | undefined,
   18 |   keys: Array<string | null | undefined>,
   19 | ) {
   20 |   if (!cuesByRecordingId) return (keys.find(Boolean) as string | null) ?? null;
   21 |   for (const k of keys) {
   22 |     if (!k) continue;
   23 |     const xs = cuesByRecordingId[k];
   24 |     if (Array.isArray(xs) && xs.length) return k;
   25 |   }
   26 |   return (keys.find(Boolean) as string | null) ?? null;
   27 | }
   28 | 
   29 | function StageCore(props: {
   30 |   variant: "inline" | "fullscreen";
   31 |   cuesByRecordingId?: CuesByRecordingId;
   32 |   offsetByRecordingId?: OffsetByRecordingId;
   33 |   offsetMs?: number;
   34 |   autoResumeOnSeek?: boolean;
   35 |   lyricsMode?: "embedded" | "none";
   36 | }) {
   37 |   const {
   38 |     variant,
   39 |     cuesByRecordingId: cuesByRecordingIdProp,
   40 |     offsetByRecordingId: offsetByRecordingIdProp,
   41 |     offsetMs: globalOffsetMs = 0,
   42 |     autoResumeOnSeek = false,
   43 |     lyricsMode = "embedded",
   44 |   } = props;
   45 | 
   46 |   const player = usePlayerVisual();
   47 |   const actions = usePlayerActions();
   48 |   const snap = useLyricsSnapshot();
   49 | 
   50 |   // ✅ prefer props when provided; otherwise use lyricsSurface
   51 |   const cuesByRecordingId = cuesByRecordingIdProp ?? snap.cuesByRecordingId;
   52 |   const offsetByRecordingId =
   53 |     offsetByRecordingIdProp ?? snap.offsetByRecordingId;
   54 | 
   55 |   // Register stage presence; fullscreen wins if it exists.
   56 |   React.useEffect(() => mediaSurface.registerStage(variant), [variant]);
   57 | 
   58 |   const [surfaceRecordingId, setSurfaceRecordingId] = React.useState<
   59 |     string | null
   60 |   >(() => mediaSurface.getRecordingId());
   61 | 
   62 |   React.useEffect(() => {
   63 |     const unsub = mediaSurface.subscribe((e) => {
   64 |       if (e.type === "track") setSurfaceRecordingId(e.id);
   65 |     });
   66 |     return unsub;
   67 |   }, []);
   68 | 
   69 |   const playerRecordingId = player.current?.recordingId ?? null;
   70 |   const playerDisplayId = player.current?.displayId ?? null;
   71 |   const playerMuxId = player.current?.muxPlaybackId ?? null;
   72 | 
   73 |   const recordingId = pickKeyWithCues(cuesByRecordingId, [
   74 |     playerRecordingId,
   75 |     surfaceRecordingId,
   76 |     playerMuxId,
   77 |   ]);
   78 | 
   79 |   const displayId = playerDisplayId;
   80 | 
   81 |   const cues: LyricCue[] | null = recordingId
   82 |     ? Array.isArray(cuesByRecordingId?.[recordingId]) &&
   83 |       cuesByRecordingId[recordingId].length
   84 |       ? cuesByRecordingId[recordingId]
   85 |       : null
   86 |     : null;
   87 | 
   88 |   const trackOffsetMs =
   89 |     recordingId && typeof offsetByRecordingId?.[recordingId] === "number"
   90 |       ? Number.isFinite(offsetByRecordingId[recordingId])
   91 |         ? offsetByRecordingId[recordingId]
   92 |         : 0
   93 |       : 0;
   94 | 
   95 |   const effectiveOffsetMs = trackOffsetMs + globalOffsetMs;
   96 |   const resumeTrack = player.current ?? player.firstQueuedTrack;
   97 | 
   98 |   const onSeek = React.useCallback(
   99 |     (tMs: number) => {
  100 |       const ms = Math.max(0, Math.floor(tMs));
  101 |       actions.seek(ms);
  102 | 
  103 |       if (autoResumeOnSeek) {
  104 |         if (!resumeTrack) return;
  105 |         actions.setIntent("play");
  106 |         actions.play(resumeTrack);
  107 |         window.dispatchEvent(new Event("af:play-intent"));
  108 |       }
  109 |     },
  110 |     [actions, autoResumeOnSeek, resumeTrack],
  111 |   );
  112 | 
  113 |   const lyricsVariant = variant === "inline" ? "inline" : "stage";
  114 |   const reservedBottomPx = variant === "inline" ? 0 : STAGE_TRANSPORT_FOOTER_PX;
  115 | 
  116 |   return (
  117 |     <div
  118 |       style={{
  119 |         position: "absolute",
  120 |         inset: 0,
  121 |         borderRadius: 18,
  122 |         overflow: "hidden",
  123 |         background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.35)",
  124 |         isolation: "isolate",
  125 |       }}
  126 |     >
  127 |       <div style={{ position: "absolute", inset: 0, zIndex: 0 }}>
  128 |         <VisualizerCanvas variant={variant} />
  129 |       </div>
  130 | 
  131 |       <div
  132 |         aria-hidden="true"
  133 |         style={{
  134 |           position: "absolute",
  135 |           inset: 0,
  136 |           zIndex: 1,
  137 |           background:
  138 |             variant === "inline"
  139 |               ? "radial-gradient(70% 55% at 50% 40%, rgba(0,0,0,0.00), rgba(0,0,0,0.22) 72%), linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(0,0,0,0.14), rgba(0,0,0,0.06) 45%, rgba(0,0,0,0.16))"
  140 |               : "radial-gradient(70% 55% at 50% 40%, rgba(0,0,0,0.00), rgba(0,0,0,0.28) 72%), linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(0,0,0,0.22), rgba(0,0,0,0.10) 45%, rgba(0,0,0,0.24))",
  141 |           pointerEvents: "none",
  142 |         }}
  143 |       />
  144 | 
  145 |       <div style={{ position: "absolute", inset: 0, zIndex: 2 }}>
  146 |         {lyricsMode === "embedded" ? (
  147 |           <LyricsOverlay
  148 |             recordingId={recordingId}
  149 |             displayId={displayId}
  150 |             cues={cues}
  151 |             offsetMs={effectiveOffsetMs}
  152 |             onSeek={onSeek}
  153 |             variant={lyricsVariant}
  154 |             reservedBottomPx={reservedBottomPx}
  155 |           />
  156 |         ) : null}
  157 |       </div>
  158 |     </div>
  159 |   );
  160 | }
  161 | 
  162 | export default React.memo(StageCore);

   65 | function useIdleCursor(active: boolean, timeoutMs: number) {
   66 |   const [hidden, setHidden] = React.useState(false);
   67 | 
   68 |   React.useEffect(() => {
   69 |     if (!active) {
   70 |       setHidden(false);
   71 |       return;
   72 |     }
   73 | 
   74 |     let timer: number | null = null;
   75 | 
   76 |     const reset = () => {
   77 |       setHidden(false);
   78 |       if (timer != null) window.clearTimeout(timer);
   79 |       timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
   80 |         setHidden(true);
   81 |       }, timeoutMs);
   82 |     };
   83 | 
   84 |     reset();
   85 | 
   86 |     const onMove = () => reset();
   87 |     const onDown = () => reset();
   88 | 
   89 |     window.addEventListener("mousemove", onMove, { passive: true });
   90 |     window.addEventListener("mousedown", onDown, { passive: true });
   91 | 
   92 |     return () => {
   93 |       if (timer != null) window.clearTimeout(timer);
   94 |       window.removeEventListener("mousemove", onMove);
   95 |       window.removeEventListener("mousedown", onDown);
   96 |     };
   97 |   }, [active, timeoutMs]);
   98 | 
   99 |   return hidden;

  291 | export default function StageInline(props: { height?: number }) {
  292 |   const { height = 300 } = props;
  293 |   const p = usePlayer();
  294 | 
  295 |   // Lazy-load lyrics for the currently playing track when missing.
  296 |   const currentRecordingId = p.current?.recordingId ?? null;
  297 | 
  298 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  299 |     if (!currentRecordingId) return;
  300 | 
  301 |     const recordingId = currentRecordingId; // stable capture
  302 |     const ac = new AbortController();
  303 | 
  304 |     void ensureLyricsForTrack(recordingId, { signal: ac.signal });
  305 | 
  306 |     return () => ac.abort();
  307 |   }, [currentRecordingId]);
  308 | 
  309 |   const isMobile = useIsMobile(640);
  310 |   const isAdmin = useIsAdminBodyFlag();
  311 |   const inlineHeight = isMobile
  312 |     ? Math.max(140, Math.round(height * 0.5))
  313 |     : height;
  314 | 
  315 |   const [mounted, setMounted] = React.useState(false);
  316 |   React.useEffect(() => setMounted(true), []);
  317 | 
  318 |   const [open, setOpen] = React.useState(false);
  319 |   const enableIdleCursor = open && !isMobile;
  320 |   const cursorHidden = useIdleCursor(enableIdleCursor, 3000);
  321 | 
  322 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  323 |     lockBodyScroll(open);
  324 |     return () => lockBodyScroll(false);
  325 |   }, [open]);
  326 | 
  327 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  328 |     if (!open) return;
  329 |     if (typeof document === "undefined") return;
  330 | 
  331 |     const exitFsIfNeeded = async () => {
  332 |       try {
  333 |         if (
  334 |           document.fullscreenElement &&
  335 |           typeof document.exitFullscreen === "function"
  336 |         ) {
  337 |           await document.exitFullscreen();
  338 |         }
  339 |       } catch {
  340 |         // ignore
  341 |       }
  342 |     };
  343 | 
  344 |     const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
  345 |       if (e.key !== "Escape") return;
  346 |       e.preventDefault();
  347 |       void exitFsIfNeeded().finally(() => setOpen(false));
  348 |     };
  349 | 
  350 |     const onFsChange = () => {
  351 |       if (!document.fullscreenElement) setOpen(false);
  352 |     };
  353 | 
  354 |     window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  355 |     document.addEventListener("fullscreenchange", onFsChange);
  356 |     return () => {
  357 |       window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  358 |       document.removeEventListener("fullscreenchange", onFsChange);
  359 |     };
  360 |   }, [open]);
  361 | 
  362 |   const tryRequestFullscreen = React.useCallback(async () => {
  363 |     if (typeof document === "undefined") return;
  364 |     const el = document.getElementById("af-stage-overlay");
  365 |     if (!el) return;
  366 |     const requestFullscreen = (
  367 |       el as Element & { requestFullscreen?: () => Promise<void> }
  368 |     ).requestFullscreen;
  369 |     if (typeof requestFullscreen !== "function") return;
  370 |     try {
  371 |       await requestFullscreen.call(el);
  372 |     } catch {
  373 |       // ignore
  374 |     }
  375 |   }, []);
  376 | 
  377 |   const nothingPlaying = p.queue.length === 0 && !p.current?.recordingId;
  378 | 
  379 |   const handleOverlayBackdropMouseDown = React.useCallback(
  380 |     (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
  381 |       if (e.target === e.currentTarget) setOpen(false);
  382 |     },
  383 |     [],
  384 |   );
  385 | 
  386 |   const handleOverlayClose = React.useCallback(() => {
  387 |     setOpen(false);
  388 |   }, []);
  389 | 
  390 |   const handleOverlayRequestFullscreen = React.useCallback(() => {
  391 |     void tryRequestFullscreen();
  392 |   }, [tryRequestFullscreen]);
  393 | 
  394 |   const overlay =
  395 |     mounted && open
  396 |       ? createPortal(
  397 |           <FullscreenStageOverlay
  398 |             cursorHidden={cursorHidden}
  399 |             isMobile={isMobile}
  400 |             showPerfHud={isAdmin}
  401 |             onBackdropMouseDown={handleOverlayBackdropMouseDown}
  402 |             onRequestFullscreen={handleOverlayRequestFullscreen}
  403 |             onClose={handleOverlayClose}
  404 |           />,
  405 |           document.body,
  406 |         )
  407 |       : null;
  408 | 
  409 |   return (
  410 |     <>
  411 |       <div
  412 |         style={{
  413 |           borderRadius: 18,
  414 |           border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.12)",
  415 |           background: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)",
  416 |           overflow: "hidden",
  417 |           height: inlineHeight,
  418 |           position: "relative",
  419 |         }}
  420 |       >
  421 |         {!open ? (
  422 |           <div style={{ position: "absolute", inset: 0 }}>
  423 |             <StageCore variant="inline" lyricsMode="embedded" />
  424 |           </div>
  425 |         ) : null}
  426 | 
  427 |         <div
  428 |           aria-hidden
  429 |           style={{
  430 |             position: "absolute",
  431 |             left: 0,
  432 |             right: 0,
  433 |             top: 0,
  434 |             height: 56,
  435 |             background:
  436 |               "linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(0,0,0,0.45), rgba(0,0,0,0.18) 55%, rgba(0,0,0,0.00))",
  437 |             pointerEvents: "none",
  438 |           }}
  439 |         />
  440 | 
  441 |         <div
  442 |           style={{
  443 |             position: "absolute",
  444 |             top: 10,
  445 |             right: 10,
  446 |             pointerEvents: "auto",
  447 |             zIndex: 50,
  448 |           }}
  449 |         >
  450 |           <RoundIconButton
  451 |             label="Open stage fullscreen"
  452 |             title={nothingPlaying ? "Nothing playing" : "Open fullscreen stage"}
  453 |             disabled={nothingPlaying}
  454 |             onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
  455 |           >
  456 |             <IconFullscreen />
  457 |           </RoundIconButton>
  458 |         </div>
  459 |       </div>
  460 | 
  461 |       {overlay}
  462 |     </>
  463 |   );
  464 | }
